# Get-Rescue Ticket Widget — Working Log

This file is the running record for `app/widget.html` — a Zoho Creator embedded widget that lets an agent create and drive a Get-Rescue (RSR/TOW dispatch) ticket step-by-step. **Read this file first before making changes** — it tracks what's confirmed against the live Zoho account vs. what's still an assumption, has a code map so you don't have to re-read the whole file to know what exists, and a dated log of what's changed.

Update this file whenever a change is made or a schema fact gets confirmed/corrected via live testing. Line numbers below drift as the file changes — if a number looks off, `grep -n` the symbol name rather than assuming the file moved.

See also **`QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md`** (same folder) — a short, current punch list of things that need the client's input or Zoho Studio access to answer, kept separate from this file's much longer §4 so it doesn't get lost in the narrative.

See also **`../FIELDS.md`** (repo root) — the single source of truth for every field API name used across *all* the Get-Rescue widgets (this one plus Technician/Driver/Vendor), organized step-by-step through the ticket's lifecycle. If a field name here ever disagrees with `FIELDS.md`, `FIELDS.md` wins — that's where corrections get made first.

See also **`../ACCESS.md`** (repo root) — which Zoho Creator Forms/Reports this (and every other) widget needs permission to.

---

## 1. What this is

- One HTML/CSS/JS file (`app/widget.html`), embedded as a Zoho Creator widget.
- Zoho Creator is used only as the backend, via the widget SDK's Data API (`getRecords`/`addRecords`/`updateRecordById`) — no `ZOHO.CREATOR.init()` in this SDK build; see `resolve()` for the namespace-scanning pattern used instead.
- **Everything for one ticket lives in a single `Create_Case` record** — added on first save, updated on every subsequent step. `Create_Case.Status` is a real fixed picklist (confirmed 2026-07-28) — every stage writes one of its exact 16 values, never a human-readable stage title.
- A step wizard (`STAGES` array) walks: Create → Quote → Booking Fee → Capture Locations → Vendor Assignment → branch on Service_Type → RSR path *or* TOW path → Final Payment → Final Closure.
- Falls back to demo/sample data (`DEMO`, `DEMO_VEHICLES`, `DEMO_ISSUES`) when the Creator SDK isn't detected, so it's previewable in a plain browser.
- File layout: CSS in `<style>` (~lines 1–195), HTML body/markup (~197–267), all JS in one `<script>` (~268–end).

---

## 2. Code map (read this instead of re-reading the whole file)

### CONFIG (top of the `<script>`, ~line 279)
```
appName: "get-rescue"                 formName: "Create_Case"          reportName: "Agent_Ticket_Report"
phoneField: "Phone_Number"            stageField: "Status"             caseIdField: "Case_ID"
searchTextFields: ["Case_ID","Vehicle_Registration_Number","Client_Tracking_Id"]  (OR'd with phoneField in the topbar search — see §5)
serviceTypeField: "Service_Type"      nameField: "Customer_Name"       vehicleField: "Vehicle"
vehicleCategoryField: "Category"      fetchLatest: 1000 (maxed out 2026-07-28, was 200 — see §5)
rateMasterReport: "Rate_Master_Report" (best-guess report name — see §4)

optionReports (master tables the widget pulls picker options from — separate from the Create_Case field names above):
  vehicles    -> vehicle_master_Report  (label: Name, max 1000)
  issues      -> Vehicle_Issue_Report   (label: Issue_Name/Name)
  vendors     -> vendors_Report         (label: Vendor_Name/Name/vendor_name)
  technicians -> technicians_Report     (label: technician_name/Name, emailField: Email)
  clients     -> Client_Report          (label: Client_Name/Name)

vendorDistanceKm: { RSR:8, TOW:20 }  (Assignment step's "Preferred_Vendor = No" radius filter, resolved per-ticket by radiusKmFor() — see §5, 2026-07-30 / split by Service_Type 2026-07-31)
vendorFieldCandidates (added 2026-07-30, see §4 — NOT confirmed live, unlike the label
candidates above): phone/priority/status/lat/lon field-name guesses tried in order
against each vendors_Report record — degrades to blank/unknown if none match.
```

### Function index

| Function | ~Line | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `resolve(method)` | 303 | Finds an SDK method across `ZOHO.CREATOR.{DATA,PUBLISH,META,UTIL,FILE}` — this SDK build has no `.init()` |
| `apiGet` / `apiGetReport` / `apiAdd` / `apiUpdate` / `apiUpload` | 311–341 | Thin wrappers over `getRecords`/`addRecords`/`updateRecordById`/`uploadFile` |
| `buildPath(serviceType)` | 557 | Concatenates `all` + (`rsr`\|`tow`) + `pay` + `final` branch stages into the active wizard path |
| `computeStatus(step, record)` | 568 | Resolves a stage's `status` (fixed string or `(record)=>string` for branching stages) — this is what gets written to `Create_Case.Status` |
| `quoteStatus(record)` | ~374 | The `quote` stage's branching status logic — see the STAGES table above for the exact rules. Also used directly by its `canAdvance` check (not just via `computeStatus`) |
| `BUCKETS` / `bucketOfStatus(status)` | ~588/598 | The **4** dashboard report lists (was 3 — see §5) — each has a literal `statuses` array, an `accent` color, a `pill` class, and an inline SVG `icon`: `New & Open`=`[NEW,OPEN,SERVICE FEE QUOTE,BOOKING FEE PENDING,LOCATION REQUEST,LOCATION PENDING]`, **`Ready & Invited`** (renamed 2026-07-28, was "Ready & Assign")=`[SCHEDULED,READY,RSP REJECT,RSP COMPLETE]`, `Work In Progress`=`[RSP ON THE WAY,WORK IN PROGRESS,LOADING,TO DROP LOCATION,UNLOADING]`, **`RSP Closed`** (renamed 2026-07-28, was "Closed")=`[RSP CANCELLED,CX REJECT,RSP CLOSED]`. `bucketOfStatus` returns the matching index or **-1** if the Status isn't in any of the four — those tickets simply don't show on the dashboard (see §4 — notably the literal `CLOSED` status isn't in any bucket's list) |
| `stageIndexForStatus(path, status)` | 587 | Finds which stage a reopened ticket should resume at, from its saved Status (best-fit — see §4) |
| `loadOptions()` | 595 | Pulls vehicles/issues/vendors/technicians/clients option lists at boot |
| `optionsListFor(field)` / `resolveOptions(field)` | 614/620 | Resolves a field's `o` spec (`@vehicles`, `@vehicleIssues`, a literal array, etc.) to a `{v,label,id,raw}` option list |
| `valuesOf(v)` | 628 | Normalizes a Zoho field value (string/array/lookup-object) into a plain string array — used everywhere a multi-select or lookup value needs comparing |
| `vehicleIssuesFor(vehicleLabel)` | 634 | Filters `Vehicle_Issue_Report` (`OPTIONS.issues`) to rows whose `VEHICLE_TYPE` matches the selected vehicle's `Category` |
| `isTowIssue(issueLabel)` | 645 | True if the given issue's `Issue_Type` contains "TOW" — drives the silent `Service_Type` derivation |
| `OFFICE_LAT`/`OFFICE_LON` / `haversineKm()` / `calcRoundTripKm(r)` | ~680–697 | Static office coordinates (`12.967827711025203, 77.611228` — interim, see §4) + a plain Haversine great-circle distance. `calcRoundTripKm` sums office→breakdown→drop→office (or office→breakdown→office if no drop pin yet) for the TOW-only `Calculate_Distance` button on the `locations` stage |
| `firstRawValue()` / `vendorInfo(opt)` / `vendorDistanceKm()` / `vendorsFor(record)` / `vendorPickerHint()` / `vendorOptionHtml()` / `formatVendorForSave()` | ~869–957 (added 2026-07-30, see §5) | The `assignment` stage's `Vendors` picker. `vendorInfo` pulls each vendor's phone/priority/online-status/lat-lon off `vendors_Report` via `CONFIG.vendorFieldCandidates` (tries several field-name guesses, degrades gracefully — **not confirmed live**, see §4). `vendorDistanceKm` is the same plain Haversine distance `calcRoundTripKm` uses, from the ticket's own `Latitude`/`Longitude` (set on the `locations` stage) to the vendor's own lat/lon. `vendorsFor(record)` is the field's actual option-list resolver (`o:"@vendorsFiltered"`, wired into `optionsListFor`): `Preferred_Vendor==="Yes"` → every vendor; otherwise → only those within `CONFIG.vendorDistanceKm` (8, falls back to showing everyone if no vendor/ticket location data is available rather than an empty list). `vendorPickerHint` drives the field's dynamic `hint` line explaining which mode is active. `vendorOptionHtml` renders each dropdown row (name, priority, distance, phone, Online/Offline flag — reuses the dashboard's `.flag` chip look). `formatVendorForSave` composes the confirmed save format (see §3) on write; `openTicket` reverses it (via the existing `vendorDisplayName`) on read so reopening a ticket shows a plain-name chip again |
| `RATE_MASTER` / `calcQuote()` | ~672/673 | `RATE_MASTER` is all `Rate_Master_Report` rows, fetched at boot. `calcQuote()` ports the native form's fee-calc Deluge: matches each selected `Vehicle_Issue` against `Vehicle_Type`+`Vehicle_Segment`+`Issue.Issue_Name`, sums `Fixed_Rate` or a distance formula, returns `{total, breakup, needsDistance}` |
| `getTickets` / `addTicket` / `updateTicket` / `demoQuery` | 679–685 | Real-vs-demo data switch (`SDK_READY` flag decides). `demoQuery` is a best-effort stand-in for a real Creator criteria query — pulls every quoted term back out of the built criteria string (one per OR'd `.contains()` clause) and checks each `DEMO` record's phone digits + `CONFIG.searchTextFields` against them (updated 2026-07-28 for the multi-field search — see §5) |
| `readVal(rec, key)` | 697 | Unwraps a Zoho field value (lookup object, name object, array) into a display string — used for rendering, not for payload building |
| `caseLabel(rec)` | 698 | `Case_ID` if present, else `#<recordID>` |
| `fmtDateTime12(d)` / `parseSlotStart(slot)` / `computeServiceTime(r)` | ~763–783 | Compute `Computed_Service_Time` (12-hour + AM/PM + seconds format) — `NOW` → +30 min from save time, `LATER` → `Schedule_Date` + the picked `Service_Time` slot's start |
| `formatIndianPhone(v)` | ~765 | Prepends `+91` to any `t:"tel"` field value with no country code (applied in `collectStep`) |
| `loadDashboard` / `renderReportLists` / `rowHtml` / `initials` | ~843–885 | Dashboard is 4 stacked list sections (not a 4-column kanban — changed 2026-07-28; was 3 lists until later the same day when the 4th, RSP Closed, was added — see §5), **every** matching ticket per list (was capped at the latest 5 — removed the `slice(0,5)` later the same day, see §5). `rowHtml` renders one ticket row (avatar initials, Case ID, name, vehicle/issue, status pill); also reused by search results. Two conditional extras are gated by which `BUCKETS` entry the row's own Status resolves to (via `bucketOfStatus`, matched by bucket **name**, not index) — **not** shown on every row: |
| `isPaidFlag(r)` / `hasLocFlag(r)` / `FLAG_ON` / `FLAG_OFF` | ~953 | The "Paid"/"Loc" checkbox chips — **New & Open rows only** (`bucket.name==="New & Open"`, added 2026-07-28, restricted to just this bucket later the same day). `isPaidFlag` is ticked (green) when `Payment_Status==="PAID"` **or** `Booking_Fee` isn't `>0` (nothing to collect counts as paid — same rule `quoteStatus()` uses for its fee-zero branch). `hasLocFlag` is ticked once both `Latitude` and `Longitude` hold an actual value (explicit null/empty check first, since `Number("")` is `0`, not `NaN` — a blank coordinate must not read as valid `0,0`). |
| Vendor line in `rowHtml` / `vendorDisplayName(v)` / `vendorNamesFor(r)` | ~953 | **Work In Progress rows only** (`bucket.name==="Work In Progress"`) — shows just the vendor **name**, not the full composite `Vendors` string, as a second meta line when non-empty. `Vendors` saves as `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"` (see §3) — `vendorDisplayName` strips the leading `[n]` and everything from the first `|` onward; `vendorNamesFor` applies that to every selected vendor (it's a multiselect) and joins the names with `, `. Falls back to `Vendor_Email` if `Vendors` is empty. Added 2026-07-28; name-only parsing added later the same day (was showing the full priority/phone/status string). |
| "Service Time" meta line in `rowHtml` | ~990 | Shows `Computed_Service_Time` on every row — dashboard lists **and** search results. Added 2026-07-28 as a search-only `opts.showServiceTime` flag, then widened the same day to every row per a follow-up request ("show Computed_Service_Time in all reports also") — the `opts` param was removed again since nothing else needed it. |
| `onSearchInput` / `runSearch` / `closeSearchDropdown` | ~754 | Topbar search — reads via `getTickets`, same `Agent_Ticket_Report` as everything else. **Matches on `CONFIG.phoneField` (digits-only, needs ≥3 digits typed) OR'd with every field in `CONFIG.searchTextFields`** (`Case_ID`, `Vehicle_Registration_Number`, `Client_Tracking_Id` — added 2026-07-28, matched as-typed) — so the same box now finds a ticket by phone number, Case ID, vehicle reg. no., or client tracking ID. Typed text has `"` stripped before going into the criteria string. Query only fires on `#searchBtn` click / Enter, requiring ≥3 characters typed (else a toast) — **changed 2026-07-28: was originally a decorative (unclickable) icon + debounced live-as-you-type search rendered inline under "Live Tickets"; the button/dropdown UI was added first, the 10-digit-only gate was then widened to this ≥3-character multi-field gate the same day once Case ID/reg no./tracking ID searching was requested.** Results render in a `#searchDropdown` popover anchored under the search bar; clicking a row calls `openTicket(rec)`, identical to a dashboard row. Dropdown auto-closes on an outside click or when cleared. |
| `newTicket()` / `openTicket(rec)` | 784/799 | Start a blank wizard vs. resume an existing record (calls `unwrapLookup` on any `useId` fields, `setPath`, `stageIndexForStatus`) |
| `unwrapLookup(v)` | 793 | Reverse of the ID-mirroring below — turns a returned `{ID,display_value}` (or array of them) back into plain label(s) for the UI |
| `setPath()` | 807 | Rebuilds `state.path` from the record's current `Service_Type` |
| `renderWizard()` / `renderFields(step)` / `buildField(f)` | 812/833/841 | Draws the stepper + the current step's fields from its `fields` array; `renderWizard()` also calls `calcQuote()` whenever the current step is `quote` — `Suggested_Rate` and `Remaining_Fee` are always recomputed (they're readonly/computed), `Rate_Breakup` is now only seeded once when empty (**changed 2026-07-28**, since it became agent-editable — see §5), same as `Total_Service_Fee` already was. `buildField` also supports per-field `default` (pre-fill when empty), `disabled` (select fields), and `readonly` (plain inputs, textareas, and file inputs — extended to the latter two 2026-07-28, see §5) flags. The stepper renders steps beyond `state.maxStepIndex` locked (`LOCK_ICON`, no click handler attached) — see `state.maxStepIndex` note below |
| `state.maxStepIndex` | — | The furthest step index reached so far — set to `stepIndex` on `newTicket`/`openTicket`, bumped in `saveStep` each time it advances. `jumpTo`/the stepper's click wiring both refuse `i>maxStepIndex`, so the agent can't skip ahead to a step whose prerequisites (mandatory fields on earlier steps) haven't actually been saved yet. Going backward (`prevStep`, or clicking an earlier step) is always allowed. |
| `step.canAdvance(record)` / `step.blockedMessage` | — | Optional per-stage gate, checked in `saveStep()` in addition to (not instead of) the required-fields check. If present and it returns false, the save still persists (whatever `Status` it currently computes to), but instead of advancing to the next step, `saveStep()` closes the wizard back to the dashboard (same as Cancel) and toasts `blockedMessage` — **changed 2026-07-28: was previously a hard block that kept the agent stuck on the same page with an error toast; now it's just "save and exit," not "save and complain."** Reopening the ticket from the dashboard resumes on this same step (`stageIndexForStatus`). Once the gate passes (e.g. `quote`'s `quoteStatus()` reaches `LOCATION PENDING`), Save & Continue advances normally. Currently only the `quote` stage uses this. |
| `wireField(f, id)` / `wireMultiselect(f, id)` / `afterMultiselectChange(f)` | 905/934/957 | Attaches change handlers; multiselects (chips + "+ Add…") are their own code path since there's no single DOM element holding the value. `afterMultiselectChange` also clears `state.record.Total_Service_Fee` whenever `Vehicle_Issue` changes (added 2026-07-28, see §5) — same clear happens in the `Vehicle` select's own RERENDER branch, since changing vehicle also wipes `Vehicle_Issue` |
| `wireCombo(f, id)` / `wireComboAdd(f, id)` | ~1318/1364 | Searchable typeahead combos (added 2026-07-28, see §5) — `wireCombo` backs single-select `searchable` fields (`Client`, `Vehicle`), `wireComboAdd` backs the searchable multiselect "+ Add…" box (`Vehicle_Issue`). Both filter the option list case-insensitively on input, render matches into a `.combo-dropdown`, and use `mousedown` (not `click`) to pick an option so it fires before the input's own `blur` hides the dropdown first. On blur, `wireCombo` snaps the visible text back to whatever `state.record[f.n]` actually holds — so typing without picking never silently saves free text |
| `roundCoord(n)` | ~809 | Rounds a lat/long value to 6 decimal places (`Math.round(n*1e6)/1e6`). Added 2026-07-28 after a live "Longitude has exceeded its maximum digits" save error from a full-precision GPS paste — applied both where `Latitude`/`Longitude`/`DropLocationLat`/`DropLocationLong` are parsed from pasted text (`wireField`'s `parseTarget` branch) and again in `buildPayload` as a safety net for direct manual edits to those boxes |
| `RERENDER` (const, ~900) | — | Field names whose change forces a full `renderWizard()` (always preceded by `collectStep(false)` to avoid blanking other fields — see the 2026-07-25 changelog entry) |
| `closureFieldGroups()` / `closureSummaryHtml()` / `closureFieldRowHtml(f)` | ~1862 (rebuilt 2026-07-31, see §5 — table entry was still stale from the pre-2026-07-30 `summaryHtml()` name before this pass) | Final Closure's editable summary. `closureFieldGroups()` pulls every non-button/non-badge field straight out of `STAGES` (no separate schema to maintain), grouped by stage — agent groups (`create`/`quote`/`locations`/`assignment`) then vendor/technician/driver groups (`acceptance`/`reach`/`wip`, or the Tow equivalents, by `Service_Type`) then `payment`. `closureSummaryHtml()` renders each as a clickable `.crow` row (`closureFieldRowHtml`) and rebuilds `CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX` (field-name → field def) for the click handler to look up |
| `openFieldEditor(fieldName)` / `closeFieldEditor()` / `collectSingleField(f)` / `saveFieldEditor()` | ~1862 (added 2026-07-31) | The click-any-row-to-edit popup (`#fieldModal`). `openFieldEditor` looks the field up in `CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX` and renders its normal `buildField()` editor into the popup. `saveFieldEditor` reads it back (`collectSingleField`, a single-field version of `collectStep`'s per-type DOM logic) and writes just that one field to Zoho immediately (mirrors `buildPayload`'s encoding — multiselect/check/useId/coord-rounding) — popup stays open with Save re-enabled on failure so the agent can retry |
| `collectStep(validate)` | 985 | Snapshots the current step's DOM into `state.record`; multiselects read from `state.record` directly (chips UI has no DOM to scrape) |
| `idsFor(field, value)` | 1024 | Resolves display value(s) to record ID(s) for any field flagged `useId:true` |
| `buildPayload(step)` | 1029 | Builds the actual save payload: per-field mapping, `useId` resolution, `computeStatus()` for `Status`, and merges each stage's optional `extra(record)` hook |
| `saveStep(advance)` | 1052 | Validates → builds payload → add/update (generates `Case_ID` right after the first `addTicket`, since it needs the new record's own ID) → best-effort file uploads → advances or closes |
| `prevStep` / `jumpTo` / `cancelWizard` / `closeTicketAction` / `backToDash` | 1076–1092 | Wizard navigation + the two ways a ticket becomes `CLOSED`. **`cancelWizard` now saves before leaving** (changed 2026-07-28, was previously a pure discard) — best-effort `updateTicket` with whatever's on screen, so a status like `quote`'s `LOCATION REQUEST` gets persisted even if the agent cancels instead of saving. Applies to every stage, not just `quote` — see §4. |
| `boot()` / `enterDemo()` / `waitForSDK(a)` | 1108–1120 | Startup sequence — polls for the SDK ~8s before falling back to demo mode |

### STAGES table (the actual wizard — `key` is stable, everything else can change)

**⚠ STALE, confirmed 2026-09-12 (full-application audit) — this whole table is a snapshot from 2026-07-31 and has not been updated since, despite extensive later changes documented in the changelog below.** Treat the dated changelog entries (and the live code) as authoritative over this section for anything time-sensitive. One specific, confirmed-wrong claim: the paragraph immediately below says 8 stages "all carry `viewOnly:true`" — **this was removed from the code on 2026-08-07** ("show all the field editable in every steps so agent enter details") and replaced by the per-field `acceptanceIsAgentManual()`/`Acceptance_Mode` mechanism described in that date's changelog entry; no `viewOnly:true` remains anywhere in `STAGES` today. The paragraph is left below unedited as a historical record of what was true on 2026-07-31, not current behavior.

**`acceptance`, `reach`, `wip`, `acceptanceTow`, `reachTow`, `wipLoadingTow`, `wipReachedTow`, `wipUnloadingTow` all carry `viewOnly:true`** (added 2026-07-31, user-confirmed) — every field in these 8 stages renders locked (see `renderFields`'s `viewOnly` handling and the field-types note on `readonly` below); the vendor/technician/driver enters this data directly in their own native Zoho Creator, not this widget. Not marked this way: `payment` (open question, see `QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md`) and every other stage (`create`/`quote`/`locations`/`assignment` — genuinely agent-entered).

| key | branch | title | Status written | Field link names (`n`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| create | all | Create Service Ticket | **`SERVICE FEE QUOTE`** (changed 2026-07-31 from `NEW`, per the flow-spec's CTA note + user confirmation — `NEW` is no longer written anywhere; `create.statuses` is now `[]` since `quote` owns that value for resume-routing, see §5) | Customer_Name, Phone_Number1, Alternate_Phone_Number (re-added 2026-07-31, was removed 2026-07-25), Email, Client (Lookup, `@clients`, **required again — `req:true` removed 2026-07-28, then re-added later the same day**, see §5), Client_Tracking_Id, Vehicle, Vehicle_Registration_Number, Vehicle_Issue, Service_Type (hidden badge), Vehicle_Status (`req:true` added 2026-07-31), Time_of_service (`req:true` added 2026-07-31), Schedule_Date (`req:true` when `Time_of_service==="LATER"`, added 2026-07-28), Service_Time (same), Remarks (added 2026-07-31, editable — same field as `quote`'s own, see below) — plus `extra()` mirrors Phone_Number1→**Phone_Number**, computes **Computed_Service_Time**, and hardcodes **Lead_Source: "Call"** (no longer a visible field at all — removed from `fields` entirely 2026-07-28, see §5) (see §3). Save button reads **"Create Case"** (added 2026-07-31 — every other stage still uses the generic "Save & Continue") |
| quote | all | Service Fee Quote & Booking Fee (merged 2026-07-28 — was two steps, `quote` + `bookingfee`) | `quoteStatus(record)` — one of `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`/`LOCATION REQUEST`/`BOOKING FEE PENDING`/`LOCATION PENDING`, all user-confirmed (see §3); **explicitly kept exactly as-is 2026-07-31 despite a simpler Booking_Fee+Payment_Status-only model shown in the flow spec — see §5** | **`Total_Service_Fee`, `Booking_Fee`, `Rate_Breakup`, `Payment_Status`, and (as of 2026-07-31) `Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM`/`Remarks` are agent-editable — `Payment_Method`/`Image_Upload` stay `readonly`/`disabled`.** Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM (`recompute:true`, TOW only — **un-locked 2026-07-31, was `readonly` since 2026-07-28**, see §5), Suggested_Rate (`readonly`), Total_Service_Fee (`recompute:true`), Booking_Fee (`recompute:true`), Remaining_Fee (`readonly`, = Total − Booking), Rate_Breakup (editable — no longer stomped every render, see §5), **Send_Payment_Link** (`t:"button"`, shown only `when Booking_Fee>0`, labeled "Send Payment Link & Request Location" — its `action` hook also flips `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`, bundling both into one click/one button) and **Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location** (`t:"button"`, labeled "Request Location", shown only `when Booking_Fee` is *not* `>0` — same field as on `locations`, see below; buttons aren't part of the readonly pass, they're actions not data entry) — **exactly one of these two buttons is ever visible, never both** (corrected 2026-07-28, see §5; labels intentionally left as-is 2026-07-31 despite the flow spec showing different "Send via WhatsApp" copy — see §5). Payment_Method (`disabled` select, **user-confirmed keep as-is 2026-07-31** despite the flow spec showing a different option list), Payment_Status (editable, **user-confirmed keep `["PAID","PENDING","NOT APPLICABLE"]` as-is 2026-07-31** despite the flow spec showing "Success/Pending"), Total_Service_Fee1 (`readonly`, labeled "Total Service Fee (Payment Record)" — see §4 re: duplicate-looking field), Image_Upload (`readonly` → disabled file input, kept locked 2026-07-31 since `Payment_Method` staying locked means its spec'd conditional-mandatory rule could never trigger anyway), Remarks (**no longer `readonly` as of 2026-07-31** — same field as `create`'s own, editable on both) — `Suggested_Rate`/`Remaining_Fee` are auto-computed every visit; `Rate_Breakup` is seeded once then left alone (see §3). `step.key` is still `"quote"` — `renderWizard()`'s `calcQuote()` hook keys off that string. Also has `canAdvance:(r)=>quoteStatus(r)==="LOCATION PENDING"` — until that's true, Save & Continue still saves but exits to the dashboard instead of advancing (changed 2026-07-28, see §5); once `Payment_Status` is `PAID` (or the fee-zero location request is done), Save & Continue advances straight to `locations` (see §3) |
| locations | all | Capture Locations | **`READY FOR ASSIGNMENT`** once `hasLocFlag(r)` (Latitude+Longitude both present) is true, else **`LOCATION REQUEST`** — **renamed 2026-07-31 from `READY`/`LOCATION PENDING`** per the Action 3 flow spec + user confirmation; resume-routing also corrected the same day — `locations.statuses` is now `["LOCATION REQUEST","LOCATION PENDING"]` (both moved off `quote`'s own resume list), `READY FOR ASSIGNMENT` moved onto `assignment`'s (see below and §5) — resolves the long-open §4 question of what `READY` should mean. Has `canAdvance:(r)=>hasLocFlag(r)`, same save-and-exit-to-dashboard-if-not-ready pattern as `quote`. New `extra()` hook stamps **Location_Received_Time** (user-confirmed field name/type, 2026-07-31) the first time a location exists, never re-stamped after | Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location (`t:"button"`, unconditional here — also appears on `quote`, see above), LatLongPaste (`parseTarget`→Latitude/Longitude, **user re-confirmed 2026-07-31 this paste-and-split mechanism is intentional, not a stand-in for a native Location field**), Latitude, Longitude, Send_Link_For_The_Drop_Location* (`t:"button"` — **converted from `t:"check"` 2026-07-31**, see §5), DropLatLongPaste* (`parseTarget`→DropLocationLat/Long), DropLocationLat*, DropLocationLong*, Calculate_Distance* (`t:"button"`, `repeatable`, sets RT_KM via `calcRoundTripKm()`), RT_KM*, Remarks (added 2026-07-31, editable — same shared field as `create`/`quote`) (*TOW only — RSR's own distances live on the `wip` stage instead; **user re-confirmed 2026-07-31 Drop Location is TOW-only**) |
| assignment | all | Vendor Assignment | `RSP IRA (INVITE RESPONSE AWAITED)` — **resumes on `READY FOR ASSIGNMENT` and `RSP REJECT` too as of 2026-07-31** (see `locations`/`acceptance` and §5) | Preferred_Vendor, **Vendors** (searchable multiselect, `o:"@vendorsFiltered"` — added 2026-07-30, see §5: `Preferred_Vendor==="Yes"` shows every vendor, otherwise only those within `radiusKmFor(record)`'s radius (**8km RSR / 20km TOW as of 2026-07-31, was a flat 8km**, straight-line) of the ticket's own breakdown location; results **sorted by priority then distance as of 2026-07-31** (previously unsorted); each option shows priority/distance/phone/Online-Offline), Vendor_Email, Assignment_Sent, Assigned_Technician, Assigned_Technician_Email, Remarks (added 2026-07-31) |
| acceptance | rsr | Service Acceptance | `RSP ON THE WAY` (writes `RSP REJECT` too, but that no longer resumes here as of 2026-07-31 — see `assignment` above) | Service_Request, Assign_Technican, Reject_Reason*, Vendor_Email1, Technician_Email |
| reach | rsr | Reach Breakdown Location | `RSP ON THE WAY` / `RSP CANCELLED` | Action_field, ETA, Distance_To_Breakdown, Navigation_Link, Image_Upload2, Cancel_Reason* |
| wip | rsr | Work In Progress | `WORK IN PROGRESS` | Issue_Resolved, Image_Upload3, Image_Upload4, Expense_Amount, Expense_Photo, Office_To_Mechanic, Mechanic_To_Breakdown, Breakdown_To_Office, Roundtrip_Distance |
| acceptanceTow | tow | Service Acceptance (Tow) | `RSP ON THE WAY` (writes `RSP REJECT` too, but that no longer resumes here as of 2026-07-31 — see `assignment`) | Service_Request1, Assign_Technican1, Reject_Reason1*, Image_Upload1, Vendor_Email2, Technician_Email1 |
| reachTow | tow | Reach Breakdown (Tow) | `RSP ON THE WAY` | Odometer_reading_at_Reached_Location, Toll_Charges, Navigate_To_Breakdown_Link, Image_Upload5, Reject_Reason2 |
| wipLoadingTow | tow | Loading | `LOADING` / `RSP CANCELLED` | Pre_service_Photo, Image_Upload_On_truck, VCRF, Image_Upload6, Action_field2, Rejection_Reason1* |
| wipReachedTow | tow | Reached Drop Location | `TO DROP LOCATION` | Odometer_Reading, Drop_Location_Photo, Toll_Charges1 |
| wipUnloadingTow | tow | Unloading & Handover | `UNLOADING` | Unloaded_Images, VCRF_Image, Handover_Image, Handover_to_Name, Handover_to_Number, Handover_to_Designation, Navigate_To_Drop_Link, Breakdown_To_Drop_Distance, Drop_To_Office_Distance, Return_Journey_ETA |
| payment | pay | Final Payment Collection | **`RSP CLOSED`** (corrected 2026-07-31 from the `RSP COMPLETE` guess — the vendor/technician/driver widgets' own field-generation spec confirms this stage's entry status is `REMAINING FEE DUE`, now in `payment.statuses`, and this stage's own write is `RSP CLOSED`) | Remaining_Fee, Payment_Method1 (options/default corrected 2026-07-31 to `["Payment Gateway","Direct","Cash"]`/`"Payment Gateway"` — a different field from `quote`'s own `Payment_Method`, not touched), Payment_received, QR_Code, Send_Payment_Link1, Upload_Photo_of_receipt |

**There is no `closure`/`final` stage anymore** (removed 2026-07-30) — the linear wizard path now ends at `payment`. Closing a ticket is a completely separate "Final Closure" screen (`CLOSURE_FIELDS`, not part of `STAGES`/`buildPath`), reachable at any point in a ticket's timeline from a dashboard row action or a permanent button inside the wizard — see the 2026-07-30 changelog entry for the full writeup (fields, validation rules, TSF formula, what got removed). Its summary section (everything *above* the `CLOSURE_FIELDS` form) is a separate, later addition — see the 2026-07-31 changelog entry — that surfaces every field from *every* `STAGES` stage (agent- and vendor/technician/driver-side alike) as clickable, individually-editable rows.

`*` = conditionally shown (`when:`). Full path order: `all` stages, then `rsr` *or* `tow` stages (by `Service_Type`), then `pay`, then `final`.

### Field types (the `t:` value on each field def)
Most are self-explanatory (`text`/`number`/`decimal`/`date`/`tel`/`email`/`textarea`/`select`/`radio`/`check`/`multiselect`/`file`/`badge`/`summary`). Two more, plus two flags that apply to any plain field:
- **`button`** — an action button instead of a checkbox. Saves the same `["Yes"]`/`[]` shape as `check` fields. Two modes: (1) default/one-way (`Send_Payment_Link` on `quote`, `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` on `locations`) — click sets the field `true`, button switches to a disabled "done" state (`doneLabel`), no UI way to undo; (2) `repeatable:true` (`Calculate_Distance` on `locations`) — stays clickable forever, for actions you might reasonably redo (e.g. recalculating after fixing a coordinate). Either mode can carry an `action:(record)=>void` hook run on click *before* the re-render — `Calculate_Distance` uses this to set `RT_KM` via `calcRoundTripKm()`.
- **`parseTarget:[fieldA,fieldB]`** (a flag, not a type — put it on a plain `text` field) — on change, splits the field's value on `,` and writes the two parts into `fieldA`/`fieldB`, rounded to 6 decimal places via `roundCoord()` (added 2026-07-28 — see §5). Used by `LatLongPaste` → `Latitude`/`Longitude` and `DropLatLongPaste` → `DropLocationLat`/`DropLocationLong` (see `wireField`'s shared `recompute || parseTarget` branch).
- **`searchable:true`** (a flag on `t:"select"` and `t:"multiselect"` fields) — renders a typeahead combo (`.combo` text input + filtered `.combo-dropdown`) instead of a native `<select>`/`<option>` add-box. Added 2026-07-28 for `Client`, `Vehicle` (both `select`), and `Vehicle_Issue` (`multiselect`'s "+ Add…" box) — these lists can run into the hundreds, where a native dropdown is unusable. Implemented in `wireCombo`/`wireComboAdd` (`buildField`/`wireField`/`wireMultiselect` each branch on it); `collectStep` skips DOM-reading searchable selects entirely (same treatment as `multiselect`/`button`) since the combo's visible input holds a *label*, not the real value — `wireCombo` keeps `state.record[f.n]` current on every pick instead.
- **`row:"someTag"`** (a flag, not a type) — consecutive fields sharing the same `row` string render side-by-side in one full-width `.fld-row` (a 3-column sub-grid, `grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)`, collapsing to 1 column at the phone breakpoint) instead of each taking its own slot in the normal 2-column `.grid`. Added 2026-07-28 for `Customer_Name`/`Phone_Number1`/`Email` on the `create` stage (`row:"contact"`) — the only current user; extend to any 3-across row by tagging consecutive fields the same way (`renderFields` groups them, not the field defs themselves, so order in the `fields` array still matters).
- **`name2` was removed 2026-07-28** — `Customer_Name` used to render as separate First/Last boxes joined into one string on save; now it's a single plain `text` field (see changelog), matching the confirmed real schema (a single-line text field) more directly. All `name2`-specific code in `buildField`/`collectStep`/`buildPayload` was deleted, not just unused.
- **`readonly:true` now works on every field type, not just text-like inputs/textareas/file** (generalized 2026-07-31 for the vendor-stage `viewOnly` lock, see §5) — `select` maps it to the existing `disabled` attribute (same as `f.disabled`; `<select>` has no true `readonly`), `check` disables the checkbox, and `radio` both styles its `.opt` divs as locked (`.radio-set.locked` CSS) **and** has `wireField()` skip attaching their click handlers entirely — the divs aren't real form controls, so `disabled`/`readonly` attributes alone wouldn't have stopped clicks. Not generalized: `multiselect`/`button` (no field of either type currently needs it).

### Fields flagged `useId:true` (send record ID, not display text — see §3 confirmed facts)
**`Vehicle`** (create stage, Lookup to `vehicle_master_Report`) and **`Client`** (create stage, Lookup to `Client_Report`). Everything else — `Vehicle_Issue`, `Vendors`, `Assigned_Technician` — sends plain display text. Don't re-add `useId` to those without new live evidence (see changelog 2026-07-27/28).

---

## 3. Confirmed schema facts (verified against a live record or a live API error — trust these over guesses)

| Field / thing | Fact | How we know |
|---|---|---|
| `CONFIG.reportName` | Reads/updates/search all go through **`Agent_Ticket_Report`**, not `Create_Case_Report` (the original guess) — user-confirmed. Presumably a curated/filtered agent-facing view on the same `Create_Case` form; `updateRecordById` was switched to it too since it wasn't called out separately, but hasn't been independently confirmed to still work for writes (see §4). | User-confirmed |
| `Create_Case.Vehicle` | Real field name for the vehicle lookup. **Not** `vehicle_master` (that name never appears on a real record). Is a genuine Lookup — API wants the linked record's ID, not display text. | Live `getRecords` response + earlier "Invalid column value" error while we were sending `vehicle_master` |
| `Create_Case.Vehicle_Issue` | Plain multiselect of **text** (e.g. `["AIR FILL","BATTERY JUMPSTART"]`) — not a Lookup, no IDs. | Live `getRecords` response |
| `Create_Case.Vendors` | Plain multiselect of **text**, and the accepted format looks like a composed string: `"[3] Vendor One | +917210036790 | Online"` (priority + name + phone + status). **Implemented 2026-07-30** via `formatVendorForSave()` (see §5) — the priority/phone/status pieces come from `vendors_Report` via `CONFIG.vendorFieldCandidates`, which are field-name **guesses, not confirmed live** (see §4); if none of those candidate names match the real report, it silently falls back to just the plain vendor name (same as the old behavior), so this degrades safely but needs live verification. | Live `getRecords` response |
| `Create_Case.Assigned_Technician` | Assumed plain text (not a Lookup), by analogy with Vendors — **not independently confirmed**. | Inference only |
| `Create_Case.Phone_Number` | A required field separate from the one the form shows (`Phone_Number1`). Currently just mirrored via the `create` stage's `extra()` hook. | Live "Enter a value for Phone Number" error |
| `Create_Case.Customer_Name` | A **plain single-line text field**, not a composite Name field — sending `{first_name,last_name}` saved literally as the stringified object (visible in the UI as `{"last_name":"...","first_name":"..."}`). Originally fixed by joining a two-box First/Last UI into one string before saving; **2026-07-28: the UI itself was simplified to match** — a single `text` field, no more First/Last split (see §5). | Live record showing the broken stringified-object value |
| Phone numbers | Every phone field in this app is assumed Indian — if the agent doesn't type a country code, `+91` is prepended (`formatIndianPhone()`), formatted with no spaces/dashes (e.g. `+919696406443`). Applied to every `t:"tel"` field. | User-confirmed |
| `Create_Case.Client` | A real Lookup field to the `Client` form — its own picker, not a mirror of anything. Options come from `Client_Report`'s `Client_Name` field. Replaced the old plain-text `Client_Type` select entirely. | User-confirmed |
| `vehicle_master_Report` | Display field is **`Name`** (not `Vehicle_Name`). Has a **`Category`** field holding `2W`/`4W`. | User-confirmed |
| `Vehicle_Issue_Report` | Real report link name (not bare `Vehicle_Issue` — that guess likely caused an earlier 404). Has `Issue_Name`, a multiselect **`VEHICLE_TYPE`** (2W/4W), and **`Issue_Type`** (includes `TOW`; other values are non-TOW, e.g. `REPAIR`/`ROS`). | User-confirmed |
| Vehicle → Issue filtering | The Issue picker shows only `Vehicle_Issue_Report` rows whose `VEHICLE_TYPE` includes the selected vehicle's `Category`. | User-confirmed, implemented in `vehicleIssuesFor()` |
| Service_Type derivation | Comes from the **selected issue's own `Issue_Type`** (`TOW` → `Service_Type=TOW`, else `RSR`) — NOT from any vehicle_master column. Never shown in the UI; computed and saved silently. | User-confirmed; corrects an earlier wrong guess (see changelog) |
| `max_records` | Zoho only accepts **200, 500, or 1000** for this param — anything else 400s with code 9250. | Live API error |
| Zoho Creator widget SDK | No `ZOHO.CREATOR.init()` in this build — methods are spread across `ZOHO.CREATOR.{DATA,PUBLISH,META,UTIL,FILE}`; resolved via a namespace scan (`resolve()`). | Established at project start |
| `Create_Case.Status` picklist | The two screenshots showed 17 values (`NOT APPLICABLE`, `NEW`, `OPEN`, `SCHEDULED`, `READY`, `RSP IRA (INVITE RESPONSE AWAITED)`, `RSP REJECT`, `RSP ON THE WAY`, `WORK IN PROGRESS`, `LOADING`, `TO DROP LOCATION`, `UNLOADING`, `RSP COMPLETE`, `RSP CANCELLED`, `CX REJECT`, `CLOSED`, `RSP CLOSED`) — but **the list is confirmed larger than that**: `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`, `LOCATION REQUEST`, `LOCATION PENDING`, `BOOKING FEE PENDING` (all used by the `quote` stage) weren't in either screenshot. Treat the screenshot list as a lower bound, not exhaustive — other stages' status guesses may turn out to be similarly incomplete. | User-confirmed (screenshot) + later corrections proving it wasn't the full list |
| `quote` stage's exact Status flow | User-specified (2026-07-28), fully implemented in `quoteStatus()`: if `Booking_Fee` isn't `>0` (nothing to collect), the step shows only a "Request Location" button — clicked → `LOCATION PENDING` (can advance); not clicked (whether cancelled or saved without clicking) → `LOCATION REQUEST` (not yet ready to advance). If `Booking_Fee>0`, the one bundled button (see §2/§5) sends the payment link and requests the location together — `Payment_Status==="PAID"` → `LOCATION PENDING` (can advance); else clicked → `BOOKING FEE PENDING` (not yet ready); else stays `SERVICE FEE QUOTE` (not yet ready). "Not yet ready" no longer means blocked-on-this-page: Save & Continue still saves, then closes the wizard back to the dashboard so the agent can pick it up again later (changed 2026-07-28, see §5) — only reaching `LOCATION PENDING` makes Save & Continue actually advance to `locations`. No payment/location integration exists, so "sent"/"PAID" are both set by the agent by hand, not by any webhook. | User-confirmed |
| `quote` stage's Status | Writes `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`, not `OPEN` (the original guess). | User-confirmed |
| `Create_Case.Case_ID` scheme | Format `"RSID" + last 6 digits of the new record's own Zoho ID` (e.g. `RSID986335`) — short, and unique in practice since it derives from the record's own unique ID. Can only be set *after* the record exists, so it's written via a follow-up `updateTicket` call right after the first `addTicket` succeeds (see `saveStep()`). **That follow-up call is now wrapped in its own try/catch (2026-07-28)** — a real ticket was seen with a long `CSID...`-style Case_ID instead of `RSID...`, consistent with the follow-up update being rejected (e.g. if `Case_ID` is actually a server-side formula/autonumber field that already auto-stamps a value on create, and can't be overwritten via the API). The catch just `console.warn`s now instead of failing the whole save — if you see a `CSID...`-style Case_ID again, check the console for this warning to confirm/diagnose. | User-confirmed for the intended scheme; the failure mode is a live observation, root cause NOT yet confirmed — see §4 |
| `Create_Case.Computed_Service_Time` | Real field, stored **12-hour with AM/PM + seconds**, exact format `dd-MMM-yyyy hh:mm:ss A` (e.g. `26-Jul-2026 11:25:18 PM` — different shape from `fmtDateTime()`'s 24-hour output, hence the separate `fmtDateTime12()`). Confirmed against the exact format string shown on the native Zoho form (2026-07-28) — `fmtDateTime12()` verified to match it for midnight/noon/single-digit-day edge cases too. `Time_of_service==="NOW"` → 30 minutes from the moment of saving; `"LATER"` → the picked `Schedule_Date` + the picked `Service_Time` slot's *start* time (e.g. slot `"4:30 PM - 5:00 PM"` → `16:30:00`). Computed in `computeServiceTime()`, set via the `create` stage's `extra()` hook. `Schedule_Date`/`Service_Time` are now `req:true` when `Time_of_service==="LATER"` (added 2026-07-28) so this value can never come out blank/malformed — previously neither was required, so a ticket could save with an empty `Computed_Service_Time`. | User-confirmed; field itself already seen on a live record dump |
| Fee calculation (Rate_Master) | Ported directly from the native form's real Deluge (user pasted it). `vehicle_master_Report` has a **`Segment`** field (A–F) in addition to `Category`. `Rate_Master` rows have `Vehicle_Type`, `Vehicle_Segment`, `Issue` (a Lookup matched by `Issue.Issue_Name`), `Rate_Type` (`FIXED`/`FORMULA`), `Fixed_Rate`, `Formula_Base_Amount`, `Formula_KM_Buffer`, `Formula_Per_KM_Rate`, `Formula_Min_Amount`. Formula = `Formula_Base_Amount + (RT_KM + Formula_KM_Buffer) * Formula_Per_KM_Rate`, floored at `Formula_Min_Amount`. Implemented in `calcQuote()`. | User-pasted real Deluge from the native form's Create→Quote section-transition validation |

Status-per-stage mapping: see the STAGES table in §2 — `create → NEW` and `quote → SERVICE FEE QUOTE` are user-confirmed, the rest is a reasoned proposal (see §4).

---

## 4. Open questions / unconfirmed assumptions (don't treat as fact — verify before relying on them)

- **`Remaining_Fee` appears in two Final Closure summary groups** ("Service Fee & Booking" from the `quote` stage's own `readonly:true` copy, and "Final Payment" from the `payment` stage's editable copy — confirmed via an isolated `closureFieldGroups()` run, 2026-07-31) since `closureFieldGroups()` intentionally pulls straight from `STAGES` rather than deduping. Both rows edit the *same* underlying record field, so this is cosmetic, not a correctness bug — but because `CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX` is keyed by field name and the later group wins, **clicking either row's popup uses the `payment` stage's (non-readonly) field definition**, not `quote`'s readonly one. Harmless in practice (the field is genuinely editable via the real wizard's `payment` step already), just don't be surprised the "Service Fee & Booking" row's popup lets you type into what looks like it should be read-only there.

- **The lat/long precision cap was raised from 6 to 17 decimal places per explicit request (2026-07-30)** — but a live save then confirmed "…exceeded its maximum digits" still fires at 17. Rather than guess a fixed lower cap, saves now auto-retry at progressively fewer decimal places on that exact error (`saveWithCoordFallback()`, see §5) until Zoho accepts one. **RESOLVED for `Latitude`/`Longitude`/`DropLocationLat`/`DropLocationLong` (2026-09-11, user-confirmed)** — all 4 converted from Decimal to Single Line Text in Zoho, same fix already applied to `RSP_Start_Latitude`/`Longitude` back on 2026-08-03; Single Line Text has no digit limit, so this error class can't fire on these 4 fields anymore. `saveWithCoordFallback()`'s retry loop is left in place untouched (harmless — now just always succeeds on its first attempt for these 4, same as it already does for `RSP_Start_*`/`RSP_Drop_*`) in case a genuinely different rejection reason ever shows up. **`Cancel_Location_Lat`/`Cancel_Location_Lon` are still Decimal** (not part of this conversion) and could still hit this same error — not yet reported live, but worth converting too if it ever is.
- **`scrollToTop()`'s parent/top-frame attempts are speculative** (see §5) — they only take effect if the widget's iframe happens to be same-origin with the Creator page, which is not the normal setup for a hosted custom widget. If the "next step opens still scrolled down" symptom persists after this fix, it confirms the outer page owns the scroll and this document's own `scrollTo` genuinely can't reach it; the real fix would need whatever the Zoho Creator Widget SDK's actual resize/scroll-notification mechanism is (not identified in this codebase — worth checking Zoho's widget SDK docs directly).
- **All ten `CLOSURE_FIELDS` field link names are unconfirmed guesses** (`Refund_Due`, `Refund_Reason`, `Refund_Amount`, `Closure_Status`, `Closure_Reason`, `Cx_Feedback_Score`, `Cx_Feedback_Text`, `B2B_GST_Invoice_Required`, `GST_Number`, `Closure_Extra_Photo`, see §5's 2026-07-30 Final Closure entry) — none of them existed anywhere in this file before the Final Closure screen was built from a mock. If `Close Task` throws a Zoho save error naming a field, or a value silently doesn't stick, check that field's real link name in Studio and correct it here.
- **Final Closure's photo rows (one per photo field, inside their own stage group — see the 2026-07-31 changelog entry) only show an "Uploaded"/"Selected: <filename>" presence indicator, never an actual image preview** — this app has no existing mechanism anywhere (dashboard, wizard, or here) to read back a file field's URL/thumbnail after upload, so this isn't a new gap, just an existing one now visible in more places. Clicking a photo row's popup still lets you pick and immediately upload a *replacement* file (via the existing `apiUpload`), it just can't show what's already on file. Would need `getRecords`/the SDK to actually expose a file URL, which hasn't been explored.
- **Editing a field from the Final Closure summary saves it to Zoho immediately, independent of the bottom Close Task form/button** (`saveFieldEditor()`, 2026-07-31) — this is a deliberate difference from the rest of this widget, where every other screen only persists on its own explicit Save/Continue. If a ticket's `Status` is later locked down by Zoho once `CLOSED` (unconfirmed — no such guard exists in this widget itself), edits made here after closing would presumably still be attempted and fail; there's no code today that disables the summary's edit rows once `Closure_Status`/`Status` indicate the ticket is already closed.
- **RESOLVED 2026-07-31** (previously open since 2026-07-28): what `READY` should mean. A prior session note described the intended meaning as "READY comes when assignment and everything is done to vendor and vendor is about to start the work" — i.e. late in the flow, near/at vendor acceptance. The Action 3 flow spec instead explicitly defines this status as **"READY FOR ASSIGNMENT"**, written immediately once the breakdown location is captured — i.e. early, right *before* assignment happens, not after. Read these as describing two *different* points in the flow, not a contradiction: "vendor about to start the work" already has its own status further downstream (`RSP ON THE WAY`, written once the vendor/technician accepts — see `acceptance`/`acceptanceTow`). Implemented per the explicit spec + user confirmation (see §5's Action 3 entry) — flagging this reading here in case it turns out the earlier "vendor about to start" description was actually meant to replace this status rather than describe a separate, later one.
- **Locking `Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM` and `Payment_Method` read-only (2026-07-28, explicit request) means neither can currently be populated by the agent at all**, since nothing in the widget sets them automatically. Concretely: no `FORMULA`-rate (TOW) issue can get a real distance-based quote on this step anymore — `calcQuote()` will keep showing "enter distance — distance needed for this issue" forever, since the one way to enter that distance was the now-locked field. Implemented exactly as instructed rather than silently keeping it editable, but flagging clearly since it changes a previously-working calculation path. If a real distance value is meant to arrive from elsewhere (the `locations` step's `RT_KM`, an upstream automation, etc.) before the agent reaches this step, that hookup doesn't exist yet.
- **Root cause of the `CSID...`-style Case_ID sighting is not confirmed.** A real ticket showed a long `CSID`-prefixed Case_ID instead of the widget's own `RSID`+last-6-digits scheme. Best guess: `Case_ID` is a server-side formula/autonumber field that Zoho auto-populates on create, and the widget's follow-up `updateTicket` call to overwrite it with `RSID...` is silently rejected (now caught and `console.warn`'d instead of failing the save — see §3). Not yet confirmed by checking the actual console warning on a fresh save, or by checking the field's type in Zoho Studio. If it turns out `Case_ID` genuinely can't be overwritten via the API, the `RSID` scheme may need to move to a *different*, writable field instead.
- **"There is no OPEN status" was read as confirming already-current behavior, not a new instruction to change `create`'s status.** The user's exact words when specifying the `quote` flow opened with "there will [be] no open status... when they go to next page the ticket['s status] creates with SERVICE FEE QUOTE." Read this as: `OPEN` (the old, never-confirmed guess for the pre-merge `bookingfee` stage) is gone — true since the 2026-07-28 merge — and as a restatement that the *second* step's status is `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`. **Did not** touch the `create` stage's own status, which stays `NEW` (separately user-confirmed earlier the same day). If this reading is wrong and `create` was actually meant to skip straight to some other status, that's a real gap.
- **`cancelWizard()` now saves on every stage, not just `quote`.** The spec only described `quote`'s cancel behavior, but the underlying need (Cancel must persist an accurate `Status` snapshot, not silently discard) isn't stage-specific — generalizing it seemed like the correct, consistent choice rather than special-casing just `quote`. Means Cancel on *any* step now attempts a best-effort save first. Flag if that's too broad a behavior change for stages that weren't part of this request.
- **`Booking_Fee>0` as the zero-fee test** — "if Booking_Fee is zero" was implemented as `!(Number(Booking_Fee)>0)`, which also covers "not entered yet" (empty/`NaN`) and negative values, not just a literal `0`. Reasonable default (no fee typed = nothing to collect yet), but flag if the real intent was strictly `=== 0`.
- **`Total_Service_Fee` vs `Total_Service_Fee1` — likely-duplicate fields, relationship unconfirmed.** Both now sit on the same merged Quote & Booking Fee step, both literally meaning "total service fee." Neither auto-fills the other. Relabeled `Total_Service_Fee1` to "Total Service Fee (Payment Record)" just to reduce agent confusion — don't read that as confirmation they're actually different values; they may need to be kept in sync (or one of them may be legacy/unused) once someone with Studio access can check.
- **`updateRecordById`/`addRecords` via `Agent_Ticket_Report` — untested.** The user only said to *pull* (read) from `Agent_Ticket_Report`; switching updates/adds to it too was my inference (single shared `CONFIG.reportName`, and nothing suggested reads/writes should target different reports). Zoho's `updateRecordById` typically works through any report tied to the same underlying form, so this is *probably* fine, but hasn't been confirmed by an actual save yet — watch for a report-related error on the next save/update and revert just the write path to `Create_Case_Report` if so.
- **Manual lat/long paste is an explicitly temporary stand-in, not the intended final UX** — user's own words: "for now I will be putting up lat long like this manually... the location will get fetched automatically" (eventually, presumably via a real geolocation/WhatsApp-location-share flow). Don't treat `LatLongPaste`/`DropLatLongPaste` as the finished design — expect this to be replaced later.
- **The office coordinates are hardcoded** (`OFFICE_LAT`/`OFFICE_LON` — **corrected 2026-09-07** to `12.967945122837245, 77.6110507612277`, per the user-provided LOCATIONS-DISTANCES-DATETIME spec; the previous value, `12.967827711025203, 77.611228`, was off by ~13-20m. Same correction applied to all 5 files that hardcode this constant: `getRescueTicket`, `vendorTicket`, `driverTicket`, `technicianTicket`, `Ticket Kanban`) rather than pulled from any Zoho config/master — if the business ever operates from more than one office, or that location changes, this needs to become configurable (e.g. its own `CONFIG` entry or a lookup), not a bare constant.
- **`calcRoundTripKm()` is a straight-line (Haversine) distance, not a road-network distance** — real driving distance will usually be longer than this. Treat the computed `RT_KM` as an estimate for quoting purposes, not a precise mileage figure; the original architecture notes mention Google Maps distance calculation elsewhere in the app, which this doesn't replicate.
- **The dashboard now shows 4 Status groups, by explicit design (expanded 2026-07-28, see §5)** — New & Open, Ready & Invited, Work In Progress, RSP Closed (exact status lists in §2's `BUCKETS` entry). The only status still left out of all four is **`RSP IRA (INVITE RESPONSE AWAITED)`** (the `assignment` stage's own status) and, notably, the literal **`CLOSED`** status written by the `closure` stage itself — the "RSP Closed" bucket's list is `RSP CANCELLED`/`CX REJECT`/`RSP CLOSED` only, which doesn't include plain `CLOSED`. That may be intentional (e.g. if `closure` is actually expected to write `RSP CLOSED` in practice, not literal `CLOSED`) or an oversight in the spec — flagging rather than silently adding `CLOSED` myself. Those two statuses' tickets are still findable via phone search (unfiltered), just not browsable from the dashboard.
- **`Create_Case.Phone_Number`** — mirrored with the same value as `Phone_Number1`. Plainly a phone/text field, but unconfirmed there isn't a different expected format (digits-only vs. formatted).
- **`Vendors` exact save format** — the composite `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"` string seen on a real record looks Deluge-generated on the native form; the widget doesn't build this yet. Will likely need fixing when the Assignment step is actually tested.
- **`Assigned_Technician` format** — never independently confirmed as text vs. lookup.
- **`Case_ID` truncation-collision risk** — `"RSID"+last 6 digits of record ID` is short by design (user's explicit request) but not mathematically guaranteed unique; two records could theoretically share a 6-digit tail once ticket volume is very high. Acceptable tradeoff as specified, just don't rely on it as a true primary key elsewhere.
- **The real Create_Case form has many fields the widget never touches** — a live record dump showed extra fields like `Selected_Vehicle`, `Selected_Vehicle_Issues`, and multiple `..._Next` fields (e.g. `Create_Service_Ticket_Next`, `Task_Assignment_Next`) that look like section-navigation/helper fields driven by Deluge on the *native* Studio-built form. The widget bypasses the native form entirely and writes directly via the Data API, so these fields are left blank. Unknown whether any downstream automation depends on them being populated.
- **`Rate_Master_Report`'s report link name is a pattern-matched guess** (`CONFIG.rateMasterReport`) — not yet confirmed live, unlike the other `_Report` names which were each confirmed or corrected individually. If the fee calc silently returns "no rate rule found" for everything, check this first.
- **`calcQuote()`'s field-matching assumes plain-text/lookup-display-value equality** — `Rate_Master.Vehicle_Type`/`Vehicle_Segment` are assumed plain picklist text (matching `Vehicle.Category`/`Vehicle.Segment` exactly), and `Rate_Master.Issue` is assumed a Lookup whose display value equals `Vehicle_Issue_Report.Issue_Name` exactly (case-insensitive). Any mismatch (extra whitespace, different casing conventions between the two master tables) will show up as "no rate rule found" even when a rule exists.
- **`Total_Service_Fee` and (as of 2026-07-28) `Rate_Breakup` are only defaulted, not locked to the calculation** — each is set to the computed value only if empty, so an agent's manual edit persists across re-renders. `Suggested_Rate` is still always overwritten by `calcQuote()` on every visit to the Quote step (it's readonly/computed, matching the native Deluge's unconditional `input.Suggested_Rate = totalFee`) — only `Rate_Breakup` changed from "always overwritten" to "seeded once," since it became agent-editable.
- **`Total_Service_Fee` now re-tracks the calculation whenever `Vehicle_Issue` (or `Vehicle`, which clears `Vehicle_Issue`) changes** (2026-07-28) — it's explicitly cleared back to empty at the moment the issue selection changes, so the "seed if empty" logic above recomputes it fresh the next time the Quote step renders, instead of keeping a stale total left over from before the edit. This does mean any manual override of `Total_Service_Fee` is wiped out by a later issue change too — that's the explicit intent ("should be updated as soon as service issues updated"), not an oversight.
- **File uploads are best-effort** — `apiUpload()`'s exact expected signature for `uploadFile` is unconfirmed and never blocks a save if it fails.
- **Status mapping beyond the Create step is a proposal, not confirmed** — only `create → NEW` was explicitly user-confirmed. In particular: (a) `payment → RSP COMPLETE` is a guess — could be `RSP CLOSED` instead; (b) `SCHEDULED` isn't used anywhere yet, though `Time_of_service==="LATER"` at the Create step looks like an obvious candidate; (c) `CX REJECT` isn't modeled — there's no field/button anywhere for "customer rejected the service," so this status can never currently be written; (d) `reachTow` has no Action_field/cancel signal in its field list (unlike RSR's `reach`), so it can't currently set `RSP CANCELLED` even though a cancel is presumably possible there too.
- **Two RSR/TOW stage-ordering bugs fixed 2026-07-28** (inherited from initial build) — `reach` was sequenced *after* `wip` for RSR, and `wipLoadingTow` was sequenced *after* `wipReachedTow` for TOW. Both reordered; worth double-checking the fix reads correctly against the real flow.
- **Resuming a ticket by Status is a best-fit, not exact** — several stages can share the same Status value (e.g. both `acceptance` and `reach` write `RSP ON THE WAY`), so `stageIndexForStatus()` just lands on the *first* matching stage in the path; the agent can still jump to the right step via the stepper.
- **`vendors_Report`'s field names for phone/priority/online-offline-status/latitude/longitude are pattern-matched guesses, not confirmed live** (added 2026-07-30, see `CONFIG.vendorFieldCandidates` and §5). Unlike the report/label names elsewhere in this file, none of these have been checked against a real `vendors_Report` record yet. If the Assignment step's vendor dropdown shows no distance/priority/phone/status for real vendors (just the bare name), the report's actual field names differ from every candidate tried — check a live record and update the candidate lists. Also unconfirmed: whether `vendors_Report` even exposes each vendor's own location at all (vs. only ever the *ticket's* breakdown location) — if it doesn't, the 8km radius filter can never find a match and will always fall back to showing every vendor.
- **The 8km radius (`CONFIG.vendorDistanceKm`) is a straight-line Haversine distance, same caveat as `calcRoundTripKm`** — real road distance to a vendor will usually be larger than this, so a vendor just outside 8km by road might still pass the straight-line filter, and vice versa. Treat it as a rough proximity filter, not a guarantee.

---

## 5. Changelog

### 2026-07-24 — Initial build (inherited)
- Widget already implemented with `CONFIG`, `STAGES`, SDK `resolve()` pattern, RSR/TOW branch logic (`buildPath`), demo fallback data.

### 2026-07-25 — Create-step changes + UI bug fixes
- Removed `Alternate_Phone_Number` field.
- Added "Other" option to `Client_Type` with a free-text follow-up box.
- Raised vehicle fetch limit so all vehicles show (later corrected again, see 07-26).
- **First attempt** at auto Service_Type: derived issues from YES/NO columns directly on `vehicle_master`, guessed `FBT`/`MT` as the TOW-indicating column keys. *(Wrong — corrected 2026-07-26, see below.)*
- Made `Schedule_Date` date-only; expanded `Service_Time` to 9am–5pm half-hour slots.
- Fixed a real bug: several field changes (vehicle select, radio fields, issue toggle) called a full `renderWizard()` rebuild without first snapshotting the DOM into `state.record` — any plain text field being typed into (including Client Type's "Other" box) would revert/blank. Fixed by calling `collectStep(false)` before every such rebuild.
- Rebuilt Vehicle Issue as a Zoho-style multiselect: selected items show as removable chips + a "+ Add…" dropdown, replacing the old checkbox list.
- Removed `Service_Type` from the UI entirely (both the wizard header tag and the form badge) — it's computed and saved silently on submit, never rendered (still shown in the Final Closure summary review only).

### 2026-07-26 — Live-testing bug fixes
- Fixed `max_records` value errors (only 200/500/1000 allowed) — was sending 60 and 300.
- Fixed "Invalid column value" for `vehicle_master`: confirmed it's a Lookup requiring the record ID, not display text. Added `useId` field flag + `idsFor()` resolver + `unwrapLookup()` (for reopening tickets) — applied to `vehicle_master`, `Vendors`, `Assigned_Technician` at the time (see 2026-07-28 correction).
- Removed the now-unused `Vehicle_Issue` master-report fetch (dead code; possible source of an unexplained 404).
- **Corrected the Vehicle Issue mechanism entirely** after user clarification: replaced the vehicle_master-column-scanning approach with the real one — filter `Vehicle_Issue_Report` by `VEHICLE_TYPE` matching the vehicle's `Category`; derive `Service_Type` from the issue's own `Issue_Type`. Fixed the report name to `Vehicle_Issue_Report` (was guessing bare `Vehicle_Issue`) and the vehicle label field to `Name` (was guessing `Vehicle_Name`).

### 2026-07-27/28 — Field-name and useId corrections from a real record
- Added a `console.log` of the full save payload (and `Vehicle_Issue` specifically) in `saveStep()` for debugging.
- A real `getRecords` response revealed: (a) `Vehicle_Issue` is plain text, not IDs — removed `useId` from it; (b) the vehicle field is actually named **`Vehicle`**, not `vehicle_master` — renamed everywhere (`CONFIG.vehicleField`, field def, `RERENDER`, demo data); (c) `Vendors` is also plain text (a composite `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"` string, not yet replicated) — removed `useId` from `Vendors` and `Assigned_Technician` too, on the same evidence pattern.
- Net result: **only `Vehicle` is a confirmed real Lookup** needing `useId:true`; everything else sends plain display text.

### 2026-07-28 — Real Status picklist wired in (was writing arbitrary stage titles before)
- User shared a screenshot of `Create_Case.Status`'s actual choice list (16 fixed values) and confirmed the Create step must write `NEW`. Previously the widget wrote the human-readable stage title (e.g. `"Vendor Assignment"`, `"Work In Progress"`) — none of which matched the real picklist, so every save past the first was very likely silently wrong.
- Added a `statuses`/`status` pair to every stage in `STAGES`, a `computeStatus()` resolver, a `STATUS_BUCKET` lookup table (replacing the old title-based `bucketOfStatus`), and `stageIndexForStatus()` (replacing the old title-based resume lookup in `openTicket`).
- Fixed two inherited stage-ordering bugs found while doing this (RSR: reach-before-WIP; TOW: load-before-reached-drop) — see §4 for detail.
- **Only the Create→`NEW` mapping is user-confirmed; the rest (§2's STAGES table) is a proposal** built from the picklist's apparent lifecycle order and each stage's field semantics — needs review, especially the `RSP COMPLETE` vs `RSP CLOSED` question on the `payment` stage.

### 2026-07-28 (later same day) — Fixed a real "required field missing" save error
- Saving the Create step threw `{"Phone_Number":"Enter a value for Phone Number","Client":"Select a value for Client"}` — two required `Create_Case` fields, separate from the ones the form shows (`Phone_Number1`, `Client_Type`), were never being populated.
- Added a generic `extra(record)` hook on stage definitions (`buildPayload` merges its result into the save payload without adding any new visible field) and used it on the `create` stage to mirror `Phone_Number1` → `Phone_Number` and `Client_Type` → `Client`.
- Made `Client_Type` required client-side too, since `Client` (its mirror target) is mandatory on the server.
- `Client`'s expected value shape is unconfirmed (see §4) — if it turns out to be a Lookup rather than a plain picklist, this mirror will need to become a real picker like `Vehicle`'s.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Added a full code map (§2) to this file
- User asked for as much as possible to be captured here so future sessions don't need to re-read the whole 1100+ line file. Added: CONFIG reference, a function index with line numbers, and a full STAGES table (status per stage + every field link name per stage).
- **Keep this current**: whenever a field is added/renamed/removed, a function is added, or a stage's fields/status change, update §2 in the same edit — don't let it drift out of sync with the code.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Client_Type replaced with a real Client Lookup
- User confirmed `Client` is a genuine Lookup field to a separate `Client` form, with its own report `Client_Report` — resolves the open question from the previous entry.
- Removed the old plain `Client_Type` select (`Ondemand`/`Roadzen`/`Allianz`/`Other`) entirely. Added a new `Client` field (`useId:true`) sourced from `Client_Report`'s `Client_Name` field, following the exact same pattern as the `Vehicle` picker (own `optionReports` entry, own demo data `DEMO_CLIENTS`, resolved via the existing generic `useId`/`idsFor`/`unwrapLookup` machinery — no new code paths needed).
- The `create` stage's `extra()` hook now only mirrors `Phone_Number1` → `Phone_Number`; the `Client` mirror is gone since `Client` is now populated directly by its own field.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Wired the real fee-calculation logic into the Quote step
- User pasted the actual Deluge from the native form's Create→Quote section-transition validation. Ported its logic (not the section-navigation/Service_Type parts — those are handled differently by this widget already, see the `Selected_Vehicle`/`_Next` fields note in §4) into a new `calcQuote()` function.
- Revealed two new confirmed facts: `vehicle_master_Report` also has a `Segment` field (A–F), and `Rate_Master`'s exact field shape (see §3).
- `renderWizard()` now calls `calcQuote()` and overwrites `Suggested_Rate`/`Rate_Breakup` every time the Quote step is shown; `Total_Service_Fee` gets the same total only if it's still empty (won't stomp a manual edit). `Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM` got a new generic `recompute:true` field flag (new `wireField` fallback branch) so editing it live-recomputes without a full form rebuild losing other typed values.
- Added `RATE_MASTER` (fetched from `CONFIG.rateMasterReport` at boot) and demo data (`DEMO_RATE_MASTER`, plus `Segment` on `DEMO_VEHICLES`) so this is testable in preview mode. Verified the calc logic in isolation (FIXED-only, FORMULA-with-and-without-distance, multi-issue) before shipping.
- **`Rate_Master_Report`'s report name is an unconfirmed pattern-matched guess** — everything else in this feature is ported directly from real Deluge, so this is the main risk point; see §4.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Close Ticket hidden on Create step; Customer_Name and phone fixes from a live record
- **Close Ticket** button is now hidden on the `create` step (`step.key==="create"`) — there's no ticket to close yet before the first save.
- **`Customer_Name`** was saving as a literal stringified object (`{"last_name":"test 2","first_name":"raj dev"}` visible in the field) because the widget sent `{first_name,last_name}` to what's actually a plain single-line text field. Now joins them into one string (`"raj dev test 2"`) before saving; the two-box First/Last UI still works when reopening a ticket by splitting the saved string on its first space.
- **Phone numbers**: added `formatIndianPhone()` — every `t:"tel"` field (`Phone_Number1`, `Handover_to_Number`) now gets `+91` auto-prepended if the agent didn't type a country code, formatted with no spaces (`+919696406443`), applied at `collectStep()` time so it flows through to the field itself and any `extra()` mirror (`Phone_Number`).

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Lead_Source default, Distance-for-TOW-only, Remaining Fee on Quote
- **`Lead_Source`** now defaults to `"Call"` and is disabled (agent can't change it) — added generic `default`/`disabled` support to the `select` field type in `buildField` (a disabled `<select>` still reports its selected value via `el.value`, so `collectStep`/`buildPayload` pick it up normally with no special-casing needed there).
- **`Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM`** on the Quote step is now only shown when `Service_Type==="TOW"` (`when:(s)=>s.Service_Type==="TOW"`) — matches the real Deluge, where distance only matters for `FORMULA`-rate (tow) issues.
- **`Remaining_Fee`** added to the Quote step as a read-only computed field (`Total_Service_Fee − Booking_Fee`), recalculated in `renderWizard()` alongside the rate calc. Added a generic `readonly` flag to plain input rendering, and `recompute:true` to `Total_Service_Fee`/`Booking_Fee` so editing either live-updates it. Note: `Remaining_Fee` also exists as an editable field on the later `payment` stage — this just pre-fills it earlier, doesn't replace that.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Case_ID generation + quote-step Status correction
- **`Case_ID`** is now generated right after a ticket's first save: `"RSID" + last 6 digits of the new record's own ID` (e.g. `RSID986335`) — short and effectively unique since it derives from Zoho's own unique record ID. Since it depends on that ID, it's written via a follow-up `updateTicket` call immediately after the initial `addTicket` succeeds, in `saveStep()`.
- **Corrected the `quote` stage's Status** from `OPEN` to `SERVICE FEE QUOTE` (user-confirmed) — proves the two Status-picklist screenshots weren't the complete list; treat the other stage-status guesses in the STAGES table with the same caution until each is individually confirmed.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Computed_Service_Time
- Added `fmtDateTime12()` (12-hour + AM/PM + seconds, matching this field's real stored format — different from the existing 24-hour `fmtDateTime()`), `parseSlotStart()` (pulls the start time out of a `"9:00 AM - 9:30 AM"`-style slot string), and `computeServiceTime()`.
- Wired into the `create` stage's `extra()` hook: `Time_of_service==="NOW"` → 30 minutes from the moment of saving; `"LATER"` → the picked `Schedule_Date` combined with the picked `Service_Time` slot's start. Verified both branches plus the noon (`12:00 PM`) 12-hour-conversion edge case in isolation before shipping.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Dashboard redesigned: 4-column kanban → 3-section list view
- Replaced the 4-column card-grid dashboard with 3 stacked, full-width list sections, by explicit request: **New & Open** (`NEW`/`OPEN`), **Ready & Assign** (`READY`), **Work In Progress** (`WORK IN PROGRESS`) — each a literal Status match, not a broader "bucket" like before. `bucketOfStatus()` now returns `-1` for any status outside those three, and unmatched tickets are simply omitted from the dashboard (still reachable via phone search) — see §4 for the full list of statuses this now excludes.
- Consolidated the two previously-separate `BUCKETS` declarations (one held name/dot/pill for rendering, a different `STATUS_BUCKET` object held the status→number mapping) into a single `BUCKETS` array carrying `statuses`/`accent`/`pill`/`icon` together — removed `STATUS_BUCKET` entirely.
- Visual pass: each list section has a colored accent bar + icon + subtitle in its header; each ticket renders as a full-width row (avatar-style initials, Case ID, name, vehicle/issue, status pill, chevron) instead of a small square card. Search results now reuse the exact same row renderer (`rowHtml`, renamed from `cardHtml`) inside the same rounded-list container style, for visual consistency between the dashboard and search.
- Swapped a `color-mix()` box-shadow for a plain `rgba()` one — better compatibility with whatever webview Zoho Creator embeds, since `color-mix()` is a fairly recent CSS feature.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Send Payment Link as a button; stepper locks ahead of unsaved steps
- **`Send_Payment_Link`** on the `quote` stage is now a new `t:"button"` field type instead of `check` — a one-way "Send Payment Link to Customer" action button that switches to a disabled "Payment Link Sent" state on click. Saves the same `["Yes"]`/`[]` shape as a checkbox, so no Create_Case-side change needed. Added full support for this field type across `buildField`/`wireField`/`collectStep`/`buildPayload` (4 touch points, all verified consistent).
- **Stepper no longer lets the agent skip ahead of unsaved steps.** Added `state.maxStepIndex` (furthest step reached — set on `newTicket`/`openTicket`, bumped in `saveStep` on each successful advance). Steps beyond it render locked (dimmed, lock icon, no click handler) and `jumpTo()` itself also refuses to jump past it (defense in depth beyond just not wiring the click). This closes a real gap: the "Save & Continue" button already validated required fields before advancing, but the stepper's click-to-jump previously let the agent bypass that entirely by clicking straight to any later step.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Locations step: send-link button, lat/long paste parsing, TOW round-trip distance
- **`Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`** converted from `check` to the same `t:"button"` pattern as `Send_Payment_Link` (one-way, disables into "Location Link Sent"). `Send_Link_For_The_Drop_Location` (the TOW-only sibling) was left as a checkbox — not mentioned in the request, no reason to assume the same treatment applies there.
- **`LatLongPaste`/`DropLatLongPaste` now actually do something** — previously they were just free-text fields with a hint, nothing parsed them. Added a generic `parseTarget:[fieldA,fieldB]` field flag: on change, splits the pasted `"lat,long"` text on the comma and writes the two numbers into the named fields (`Latitude`/`Longitude`, or `DropLocationLat`/`DropLocationLong`). This is explicitly a stand-in for real geolocation capture — see §4.
- **`Calculate_Distance` now actually calculates something** — added a new `t:"button"` mode, `repeatable:true` (stays clickable, unlike the one-way send buttons — recalculating after fixing a coordinate should be a normal thing to do) plus a generic `action:(record)=>void` hook fields can run on click. Its `action` sets `RT_KM` via the new `calcRoundTripKm()` (Haversine straight-line distance: static office → breakdown → drop → office, falling back to office → breakdown → office if no drop pin yet).
- **Both `Calculate_Distance` and `RT_KM` are now TOW-only** on this stage (`when:(s)=>s.Service_Type==="TOW"`) — per explicit instruction; RSR's own distance fields live separately on the `wip` stage.
- Verified the coordinate-parsing and Haversine round-trip math in isolation (with and without a drop pin) before shipping.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Switched the read/update/search report to Agent_Ticket_Report
- `CONFIG.reportName` changed from `Create_Case_Report` to `Agent_Ticket_Report` (user-confirmed) — this is the single value `apiGet`/`apiUpdate` both use, so it now affects the dashboard fetch, phone search, and every record update.
- Only the *read* side was explicitly requested ("pull reports from this report"); moving the write side (`updateRecordById`) along with it was inference, not confirmed — see §4 if a save starts throwing a report-related error.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Merged the Quote and Booking Fee steps into one
- The `quote` and `bookingfee` stages are now a single stage (`key:"quote"` kept as-is so `renderWizard()`'s `calcQuote()` hook, which matches on that string, needed no change). Title updated to "Service Fee Quote & Booking Fee"; fields concatenated in their original order (quote fields, then the former booking-fee fields).
