- This actually matches the native form more closely than the previous two-step split did — its own Deluge (pasted earlier) shows/hides a single section literally named `Service_Fee_Quote_Booking_fee_Collection`.
- Surfaced (didn't resolve) a likely field duplication now that both are visible together: `Total_Service_Fee` (quote) and `Total_Service_Fee1` (former booking-fee step) both mean "total service fee" but neither auto-fills the other — see §4.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Payment-link vs location-link button is now conditional on Booking_Fee
- `Send_Payment_Link` (on the merged `quote` step) now only shows `when Booking_Fee>0` — no fee, no payment link to send.
- Added `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` to the `quote` step too (same real field already used unconditionally on `locations`), shown only `when Booking_Fee` is *not* `>0` — so exactly one of the two buttons is visible at a time on this step, depending on whether there's a fee to collect. Since it's the same field either way, its "sent" state is shared — clicking it here also shows as already-sent if the agent later reaches the `locations` step.
- The zero-fee test is `!(Number(Booking_Fee)>0)`, which also treats "not entered yet" as zero — see §4 if the real intent needed a stricter `=== 0` check.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Full Status flow for the Quote & Booking Fee step, gated advancement
- **Corrects the previous entry's assumption**: `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` ("Request Location") is now **unconditional** on `quote` (shown alongside "Send Payment Link" when `Booking_Fee>0`, not hidden by it as shipped a moment earlier) — the user's follow-up clarified both buttons are meant to co-exist when there's a fee, not be mutually exclusive.
- Added `quoteStatus(record)` implementing the full user-specified flow (see §3 for the exact rules) — `Create_Case.Status` for this step is now always one of `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`/`LOCATION REQUEST`/`BOOKING FEE PENDING`/`LOCATION PENDING`, derived purely from `Booking_Fee`/`Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`/`Send_Payment_Link`/`Payment_Status` — never a static string.
- Added a generic `step.canAdvance(record)` + `step.blockedMessage` mechanism, checked in `saveStep()`: the `quote` step can't be left forward until `quoteStatus()` reaches `LOCATION PENDING` — the save still persists (whatever status currently applies), only the wizard's step advancement is blocked, with a toast explaining why.
- **`cancelWizard()` now saves before leaving** (previously a pure discard, per its own confirm-dialog copy) — generalized to every stage, not just `quote`, since the requirement ("cancel without sending the location link → status becomes LOCATION REQUEST") only makes sense if Cancel actually persists the computed status rather than throwing the in-progress state away.
- `locations`'s `statuses` list now also accepts `LOCATION PENDING` for resume routing — a ticket saved from `quote` with that status reopens directly at `locations` (the next real step), not back at `quote`.
- Verified `quoteStatus()` against every combination from the spec (fee zero + requested/not-requested, fee>0 + nothing/link-sent/paid, paid-without-link-sent) in isolation before shipping.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Corrected: one button when there's a fee, not two
- Previous entry had `Send_Payment_Link` and `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` ("Request Location") both visible together when `Booking_Fee>0` — wrong. User clarified there must be exactly **one** button in that case.
- Fixed by giving `Send_Payment_Link` its own `action` hook that also flips `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` to `true`, relabeled "Send Payment Link & Request Location" — one click now does both. `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`'s own field def got a `when:(s)=>!(Number(s.Booking_Fee)>0)` guard so it only renders standalone in the zero-fee case. The two `when` conditions are exact opposites of each other, so exactly one button shows, never zero or two.
- User's framing: this bundling is a stand-in for a future payment-gateway integration that would trigger the location request automatically once payment completes — doing it by hand in one click is the interim equivalent.
- `quoteStatus()` itself needed no change — it already only inspects `Payment_Status`/`Send_Payment_Link` for the fee>0 branch, so the location flag riding along doesn't affect the computed Status.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Client is optional; Quote step no longer blocks, it saves-and-exits
- **`Client` (create stage) no longer has `req:true`.** User confirmed client selection isn't mandatory on the first page — removed the client-side requirement. Note this reverses the requiredness assumption from the 2026-07-28 "required field missing" changelog entry, but that entry was about the old `Client_Type`→`Client` mirror (now gone); no live evidence has re-confirmed `Client` as server-mandatory since it became its own real Lookup, so this is safe until/unless a save throws a "Select a value for Client" error again.
- **`quote` step's `canAdvance` gate changed from a hard block to save-and-exit.** Previously, saving without completing the required action (requesting location / confirming payment) showed an error toast and kept the agent stuck on the same page. Now it still saves (whatever `Status` currently applies — `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`/`LOCATION REQUEST`/`BOOKING FEE PENDING`) and then closes the wizard back to the dashboard, same as Cancel — no error, just an informational toast. The ticket shows up in one of the three dashboard report lists at its current status; clicking it reopens the wizard on the `quote` step (via `stageIndexForStatus`, unchanged) so the agent can pick up where they left off.
- Once `Payment_Status` is `PAID` (or, for zero-fee tickets, the location's been requested) — i.e. `quoteStatus()` reaches `LOCATION PENDING` — Save & Continue advances straight to `locations` as before. This is the only path that still auto-advances; every other outcome on this step now saves-and-exits instead of saves-and-blocks.
- `quoteStatus()` and `buildPayload()` needed no changes — this was purely a change to what `saveStep()` does with the `canAdvance` result (toast+stay → save+exit-to-dashboard).

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Search box is now a real button + dropdown
- **Root cause of "search doesn't work":** the search icon in the topbar was a plain decorative `<svg>` with no click handler — there was never a button to click. Live-as-you-type search existed via a debounced `input` listener, but results rendered inline under the "Live Tickets" heading, not attached to the search bar, which likely read as broken/missing.
- Turned the leading icon into a real `#searchBtn` button. Results now render in a `#searchDropdown` popover positioned directly under the search input (`.search{position:relative}` + `.search-dropdown{position:absolute;...}`), matching the ask for "show the ticket in dropdown of search bar."
- Search now only fires on `#searchBtn` click or Enter (removed per-keystroke debounced firing) and requires a full 10-digit number — fewer than that toasts an error instead of querying. Still reads from `CONFIG.reportName` (`Agent_Ticket_Report`) via the same `getTickets()` every other list uses.
- Clicking a dropdown row calls `openTicket(rec)` — the exact same function a dashboard/report row uses — so it opens in the edit wizard the same way. Dropdown closes on an outside click, on selecting a result, or when the search box is cleared.
- Removed the now-unused `#searchWrap` div and its inline "Search" section entirely.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Search was querying the wrong phone field
- **Root cause found:** `CONFIG.phoneField` was `Phone_Number1` — the field the *form* shows — but the console logging added a moment earlier confirmed the real `Create_Case`/`Agent_Ticket_Report` field the app actually searches on is **`Phone_Number`** (the one `Phone_Number1` gets mirrored into via the `create` stage's `extra()` hook — see the existing confirmed-fact row in §3). Searching on `Phone_Number1` was querying a field that either doesn't hold the value the same way in that report, or isn't the one meant for lookups — either way, no matches came back.
- Changed `CONFIG.phoneField` to `Phone_Number`. This affects `runSearch()`'s criteria field, `demoQuery()`'s local filtering, and `summaryHtml()`'s "Phone" row — all three read through `CONFIG.phoneField`, so no other code changes were needed.
- Added a matching `Phone_Number` value to every `DEMO` record (previously only `Phone_Number1` was set) so demo/preview-mode search still works with the corrected field name.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Dashboard expanded from 3 to 4 report lists, per user-specified status lists
- Replaced each bucket's 1–2 status match with the full user-specified list (see §2's `BUCKETS` entry for the exact arrays) and added a 4th bucket, **Closed** (`RSP CANCELLED`/`CX REJECT`/`RSP CLOSED`), with a new checkmark-circle icon and a muted/grey accent (reusing the existing `.pill.b-closed` style that was already the fallback for un-bucketed statuses).
- `bucketOfStatus()`/`renderReportLists()` needed no logic changes — they already iterate `BUCKETS` generically; only the `BUCKETS` data itself changed.
- Flagged in §4: the new Closed bucket's list doesn't include the literal `CLOSED` status (only `RSP CANCELLED`/`CX REJECT`/`RSP CLOSED`), even though the `closure` stage's own `STAGES` entry writes exactly `CLOSED` — so a ticket the agent finishes via Final Closure still won't show in any of the 4 lists. Didn't add `CLOSED` myself since the user gave an explicit, exact list; raise this if closed tickets seem to be "missing" from the new Closed section.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Added Paid/Location checkbox chips to every report row
- Every ticket row (dashboard lists **and** the search dropdown, since both share `rowHtml`) now shows two small green/grey checkbox chips: **Paid** and **Loc**.
- **Paid** ticks green when `Payment_Status==="PAID"` — or when `Booking_Fee` isn't `>0` at all, per explicit request ("if the booking fee is zero than also it will be ticked"). Implemented in `isPaidFlag(r)`, reusing the exact same fee-zero test `quoteStatus()` already uses, so the two stay consistent by construction.
- **Loc** ticks green once the record actually has both `Latitude` and `Longitude` set — implemented in `hasLocFlag(r)` with an explicit empty/null check before the `Number()` conversion (a blank string coordinate must not be read as valid `0,0`).
- Unticked state is a plain outline box (`FLAG_OFF`), ticked is a filled green box with a white check (`FLAG_ON`) — new `.rrow .flags`/`.flag`/`.flag.on` CSS, sitting as a second line under the vehicle/issue meta line in each row.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Bucket renames, Paid/Loc scoped to one report, Vendor shown on WIP rows
- Renamed the **Ready & Assign** bucket to **Ready & Invited** and the **Closed** bucket to **RSP Closed** — names only, the `statuses` arrays behind each are unchanged.
- **Paid/Loc chips now only render on New & Open rows.** Previously every row in every report showed them; per explicit request, `rowHtml` now checks `bucket.name==="New & Open"` (looked up via the row's own `bucketOfStatus()` result, not a hardcoded index — safer if bucket order changes later) before including the `.flags` block at all.
- **Work In Progress rows now show the assigned vendor** as a second meta line (`Vendor: ...`), reusing the same `readVal(r,"Vendors")||r.Vendor_Email` lookup `summaryHtml()` already used — only rendered when `bucket.name==="Work In Progress"` and the value is non-empty.
- Both gates match on the bucket's `name` string rather than a numeric index into `BUCKETS`, so renaming a bucket (like the two renames just above) doesn't silently break which rows get the extra chips/line.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Search now also matches Case ID, Vehicle Reg. No., Client Tracking ID
- Added `CONFIG.searchTextFields = ["Case_ID","Vehicle_Registration_Number","Client_Tracking_Id"]`. `runSearch()` now builds an OR'd criteria string across `CONFIG.phoneField` (digits-only, still needs ≥3 digits to be included) and every field in `searchTextFields` (matched as typed, quotes stripped first so a typed `"` can't break the criteria string).
- Widened the trigger gate from "must be a complete 10-digit phone number" to "≥3 characters typed" (any characters), since the box is no longer phone-only. Updated the placeholder text and the input's `type`/`inputmode` (from `tel` to `text`/`search`) to match — this only affects the topbar search box, not the wizard's own `Phone_Number1` field (still `t:"tel"`, unaffected).
- `demoQuery()` — previously a single-field regex hack — now extracts every quoted term from the criteria string and checks each against both the phone-digits and all `searchTextFields`, so demo/preview mode can exercise all four search paths without a live Zoho connection. Verified in isolation against sample records for phone, Case ID, and vehicle reg. no. lookups (including case-insensitive matching on the text fields).
- `Vehicle_Registration_Number` and `Client_Tracking_Id` aren't set on any `DEMO` record yet — those two search paths work in demo mode but won't return a match until a demo record actually carries a value for them.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Case_ID resilience, flags moved to a right-side column, vendor name-only, "Ticket Flow" removed, Client required again
- **`Case_ID` follow-up update is now best-effort.** A real ticket was seen with a `CSID...`-style Case_ID instead of the expected `RSID...`. Wrapped the post-creation `updateTicket(id,{Case_ID:caseId})` call in its own try/catch so a rejected write (e.g. if `Case_ID` is actually a formula/autonumber field Zoho already stamps on create) no longer surfaces as a full "Save failed" on ticket creation — it just `console.warn`s so it's diagnosable. Root cause not yet confirmed — see §4.
- **Paid/Loc chips moved from a line under the ticket info to a column on the right of it** (new `.flags-col` — `flex-direction:column`, sitting between `.main` and the status pill), per explicit request that they were "coming at the bottom" and should instead be "on the right... one after another."
- **Work In Progress's vendor line now shows only the vendor's name(s)**, not the full `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"` string it saves as. Added `vendorDisplayName(v)` (strips the leading `[n]` and everything from the first `|` onward) and `vendorNamesFor(r)` (applies that to every selected vendor, since `Vendors` is a multiselect, joined with `, `).
- **Removed the "Ticket Flow" subtitle text from the topbar brand** — now just "GET-RESCUE". Removed the now-dead `.brand b span` CSS (both the base rule and its mobile `display:none` override) along with it.
- **`Client` (create stage) is required again** — `req:true` re-added. This reverses the "make it optional" change from earlier the same day; the requiredness question has now flipped twice in one day, so treat whichever instruction is most recent as authoritative if it changes again.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Search results now also show Computed_Service_Time
- `rowHtml(r, opts)` gained an optional `opts.showServiceTime` flag — when set, adds a "Service Time: ..." line rendering `Computed_Service_Time`. `runSearch()` passes `{showServiceTime:true}` when rendering the search dropdown; the dashboard's `renderReportLists()` doesn't pass it, so the 4 report lists are unchanged — this was scoped to search results specifically, per the request.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Reverted Paid/Loc chips back to a horizontal row under the info
- User asked to put it back exactly as it was before the previous entry's change: Paid/Loc chips are back to a horizontal `.flags` row, sitting below the other info lines inside `.main` (same place they were in the original 2026-07-28 "Added Paid/Location checkbox chips" entry) — the intervening right-side `.flags-col` column layout is undone. Still New & Open rows only.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Computed_Service_Time widened to every report, not just search
- Removed the `opts.showServiceTime` gate — `rowHtml(r)` is back to a single-arg function and now always includes the "Service Time: ..." line (`Computed_Service_Time`) when the record has one, on dashboard rows and search rows alike. `runSearch()`'s call site reverted to plain `rowHtml` (no options object) accordingly.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Full responsive pass across breakpoints
- **Fixed a latent horizontal-overflow bug**: `.rrow`'s status pill (`white-space:nowrap`, `flex:none`) had no width cap, so a long status like `RSP IRA (INVITE RESPONSE AWAITED)` could force the whole row — and with it the page — wider than the viewport on narrow screens. Gave `.pill` a `max-width:140px` + `white-space:normal` so long status text wraps onto 2 lines inside the pill instead of stretching it; short statuses are unaffected (they never approach 140px). Also added `min-width`/ellipsis handling to `.nm` (customer name) and `.cid` (Case ID) and `flex-wrap:wrap` on `.top-line`, so an unusually long name or Case ID (e.g. the `CSID...`-style one seen earlier) truncates or wraps gracefully instead of overflowing.
- Added a hard safety net: `html,body{overflow-x:hidden;max-width:100%}` plus `img,svg{max-width:100%}` — since this widget runs inside a Zoho Creator iframe sized by the host app, any residual horizontal overflow would show up as an ugly scrollbar/clipped content in production, not just a cosmetic issue in a normal browser tab.
- Replaced the single `max-width:620px` breakpoint with four, layered: `max-width:900px` (tablets — just tightens outer padding, the 2-column form grid still fits comfortably down to ~680px), `max-width:680px` (phones — was the old 620px breakpoint: single-column form grid, search bar drops to its own row, sticky bar wraps), `max-width:420px` (small phones — shrinks the brand mark, avatars, row padding, and pill sizing further so content stays comfortable on ~360–390px devices), and `max-height:420px and (orientation:landscape)` (short landscape phones — un-stickies the topbar and shrinks the bottom-bar clearance so the form isn't squeezed between two fixed bars).
- Added `-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch` to the two horizontally/vertically-scrollable containers (`.steps` stepper, `.search-dropdown`) for smoother momentum scrolling on iOS Safari, and capped `.search-dropdown`'s height to `min(360px,70vh)` so it doesn't overrun short viewports (e.g. landscape phones).
- Not changed: the 2-column `.grid`/`.summary` threshold (still switches to 1 column at the phone breakpoint) and the overall component structure — this was a spacing/overflow/breakpoint pass, not a redesign.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Dashboard shows every ticket per report, not just the latest 5
- Removed `renderReportLists()`'s `cols[i].slice(0,5)` cap — each of the 4 report lists now renders every ticket whose Status falls in that bucket, not just the 5 most recent. Updated the "Live Tickets" subtitle from "latest 5 per report" to "all tickets per report" to match.
- **Bumped `CONFIG.fetchLatest` from 200 to 1000** (Zoho's max allowed value) — showing "all" per bucket would still have been silently capped by the earlier fetch-side limit once total ticket volume passed 200, so this was raised too so the display-side fix isn't undermined by a fetch-side one. If ticket volume ever exceeds 1000, this cap would need a paginated fetch instead — not implemented, flagged for future volume growth.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Lead_Source hidden from the frontend entirely
- Removed `Lead_Source` from the `create` stage's `fields` array (was a disabled `select` defaulting to "Call"). It's now hardcoded as `Lead_Source:"Call"` in the `create` stage's `extra()` hook instead — same mechanism already used for `Phone_Number`/`Computed_Service_Time`, so it still saves every time, it's just no longer rendered as a field at all (was previously visible-but-disabled, not truly hidden).
- The generic `default`/`disabled` `select` support added for this field back on 2026-07-28 (the earlier "Lead_Source default" entry) is still in `buildField`/`collectStep` for any other field that wants it — just no longer exercised by `Lead_Source` itself.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Customer Name is one field again; Name/Phone/Email on one row
- **Removed the First/Last split.** `Customer_Name` is back to a single plain `text` field (was `t:"name2"`, a two-box First/Last UI joined into one string on save). Deleted all `name2`-specific branches in `buildField`, `collectStep`, and `buildPayload` — not left as dead code. Also fixed the `DEMO` array's `Customer_Name` entries, which still used the old `{display_value:"..."}` object shape left over from before `Customer_Name` was confirmed as plain text — they're now plain strings too, matching real records and avoiding a "[object Object]" render in demo/preview mode.
- **`Customer_Name`, `Phone_Number1`, and `Email` now render in a single row** on the `create` stage. Added a generic `row:"someTag"` field flag: consecutive fields sharing the same tag get grouped by `renderFields()` into one `.fld-row` (a 3-column sub-grid spanning the full form width, collapsing to 1 column at the phone breakpoint) instead of each taking its own slot in the normal 2-column grid. These three fields use `row:"contact"`; the mechanism is reusable for any other 3-across grouping.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Guaranteed Computed_Service_Time format, made LATER's date/slot required
- Confirmed `fmtDateTime12()`'s output against the exact format string shown on the native Zoho form, `dd-MMM-yyyy hh:mm:ss A` — tested midnight, noon, and single-digit day/minute/second edge cases in isolation; all matched (e.g. `05-Jul-2026 12:05:03 AM`, `26-Jul-2026 12:00:00 PM`).
- Found and closed a gap that could break that guarantee: `Schedule_Date` and `Service_Time` (only shown/needed when `Time_of_service==="LATER"`) had no `req:true`, so an agent could save with `Time_of_service==="LATER"` and neither filled in — `computeServiceTime()` would then return `""` instead of a formatted string, meaning `Computed_Service_Time` could reach Zoho blank rather than in the required format. Added `req:true` to both (still gated by the same `when`, so they're only mandatory when actually shown) — `Computed_Service_Time` can no longer come out empty/malformed on a successful save.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Quote step locked down to 4 editable fields
- Per explicit request, only `Total_Service_Fee`, `Booking_Fee`, `Rate_Breakup`, and `Payment_Status` remain agent-editable on the `quote` step. Every other data field got `readonly:true` (number/textarea/file inputs) or `disabled:true` (the `Payment_Method` select) — action buttons (`Send_Payment_Link`, `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`) were left alone since they're actions, not data entry, and locking them would break the core booking-fee/location-request flow built earlier.
- Added `readonly` support to two field types that didn't have it before: `textarea` (used by `Remarks`) and `file` (used by `Image_Upload`, mapped to a `disabled` file input since there's no native readonly for file pickers).
- **`Rate_Breakup` becoming genuinely editable required a real fix, not just removing its `readonly`**: `renderWizard()` was unconditionally overwriting `state.record.Rate_Breakup` with the freshly-computed breakup text on every render of this step (same as the still-readonly `Suggested_Rate`) — any agent edit would've been silently discarded the next time the step re-rendered (e.g. after changing `Booking_Fee`). Changed it to seed `Rate_Breakup` only when empty, the same pattern already used for `Total_Service_Fee`.
- Dropped the now-dead `recompute:true` from `Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM` (meaningless once the field is readonly — its `onchange` can never fire from user input).
- Flagged in §4: locking `Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM` and `Payment_Method` means neither can be populated by the agent anymore, and nothing else in the widget sets them — this breaks the formula-rate (TOW) distance-based quote calculation specifically, until some other mechanism populates that distance.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Total_Service_Fee now recomputes when the issue selection changes
- **Root cause of the reported bug**: `renderWizard()`'s quote-step block only sets `Total_Service_Fee` from `calcQuote()`'s total *if it's currently empty* (so a manual edit persists across re-renders) — but that same guard also meant it stayed stale after the agent went back to the `create` step, changed `Vehicle_Issue` (or `Vehicle`), and returned to `quote`: the old total was still non-empty, so it never got recomputed.
- Fixed by clearing `state.record.Total_Service_Fee` back to `""` at the moment `Vehicle_Issue` changes (`afterMultiselectChange`) or `Vehicle` changes (its RERENDER branch, which already wipes `Vehicle_Issue`) — the existing "seed if empty" logic on the Quote step then naturally recomputes it fresh the next time that step renders. No changes needed to `calcQuote()` itself or the seed-if-empty logic — just when the field gets reset to trigger it.
- **This also means a manual `Total_Service_Fee` override doesn't survive a later issue change** — per the explicit request ("it should be updated as soon as service issues updated"), that's intentional, not a regression.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Verified quoteStatus() already matches a re-stated version of its rules
- User re-described the Quote step's status rules in their own words: "if the location is not there (breakdown lat/long not there) → LOCATION PENDING" and "if payment status is not paid → BOOKING FEE PENDING." Tested `quoteStatus()` against both in isolation — it already satisfies them, reading "location is not there yet" as "the location request was just sent, coordinates are pending" (matches `LOCATION PENDING`'s existing meaning: link sent, awaiting the actual coordinates, which get captured later on the `locations` step) and "payment not paid" as the state once the bundled payment/location button *has* been clicked (matches `BOOKING FEE PENDING`'s existing trigger). No code change made — flagging here in case this reading is wrong and a real behavior change (e.g. dropping the `SERVICE FEE QUOTE` pre-click state entirely) was actually intended.

### 2026-07-28 (later still) — Fixed: locations step wrote READY without any location ever captured
- **Real bug, confirmed live via screenshot**: a ticket ("raj test 5") showed `Status=READY` on the dashboard despite no breakdown location having been entered — because the `locations` step's `status` was a static `"READY"` written on every save, unconditionally, and neither `Latitude` nor `Longitude` was ever `req:true`.
- Fixed: `locations`'s `status` is now `(r)=> hasLocFlag(r) ? "READY" : "LOCATION PENDING"` — reusing the exact same `hasLocFlag()` check (`Latitude`+`Longitude` both present, with the same empty-string guard) already backing the dashboard's "Loc" chip, so the two can't disagree with each other. Added a matching `canAdvance:(r)=>hasLocFlag(r)` + `blockedMessage`, giving this step the same save-and-exit-to-dashboard-if-not-ready behavior the `quote` step already has, instead of silently advancing/mislabeling.
- **Deliberately narrow fix, by explicit user choice**: offered three options (READY moves to vendor-acceptance, READY moves to assignment-completion, or just gate today's bug) — user picked the narrow gate. `READY`'s deeper intended meaning ("assignment done, vendor about to start work") is still not wired up anywhere; flagged in §4 as a real open question, not resolved here.

### 2026-07-30 — Searchable Client/Vehicle/Vehicle_Issue dropdowns + lat/long precision fix
- **Added `searchable:true` combo dropdowns** for `Client`, `Vehicle` (both `select`), and `Vehicle_Issue` (`multiselect`'s "+ Add…" box) — these option lists (especially `vehicle_master_Report`, up to 1000 rows) were unusable as native `<select>` dropdowns. New `.combo`/`.combo-dropdown`/`.combo-opt` UI: a text input filters the option list case-insensitively as you type, matches render in a positioned dropdown below, and clicking one selects it. Implemented via new `wireCombo`/`wireComboAdd` functions plus branches in `buildField`/`wireField`/`wireMultiselect`/`collectStep` — every other `select`/`multiselect` field (no `searchable` flag) renders exactly as before, untouched.
- `Vehicle`'s combo still triggers the same RERENDER side effects as the old native select (clearing `Vehicle_Issue`/`Service_Type`/`Total_Service_Fee`) — that logic was duplicated into `wireCombo`'s pick handler rather than shared, since the two code paths (native `<select>` onchange vs. combo option click) don't share a single event to hook into.
- **Fixed a live save error**: pasting a full-precision GPS coordinate (e.g. `12.953876646990766, 77.60559707011676`) into `LatLongPaste` threw `["Longitude has exceeded its maximum digits."]` on save. Added `roundCoord()` (rounds to 6 decimal places) applied both when parsing pasted lat/long text and again in `buildPayload` as a safety net for direct manual edits to the `Latitude`/`Longitude`/`DropLocationLat`/`DropLocationLong` boxes. **Not confirmed against the field's actual configured digit limit** — 6 decimals is a reasoned guess (standard GPS precision convention); flagged in §4 in case the limit turns out to be stricter.

### 2026-07-30 (later same day) — Assignment step: distance/priority/phone/status-aware vendor picker
- **`Vendors` (on the `assignment` stage) is now a searchable multiselect** (`o:"@vendorsFiltered"`, `searchable:true`) instead of a plain native-select multiselect, per explicit request: `Preferred_Vendor==="Yes"` shows every vendor; `"No"` (or unset) filters to only vendors within a new `CONFIG.vendorDistanceKm` (8) of the ticket's own captured breakdown location (`Latitude`/`Longitude`, set on the `locations` stage) — a plain Haversine straight-line distance via the existing `haversineKm()`, no Google Maps API. If neither the ticket nor any loaded vendor has usable location data yet, it falls back to showing every vendor rather than an empty list. New `vendorsFor(record)` implements this and is wired into `optionsListFor` as a new `"@vendorsFiltered"` option-source tag (alongside the existing `"@vehicleIssues"` one).
- **Each dropdown option now shows the vendor's name, priority, computed distance, phone, and an Online/Offline flag** (`vendorOptionHtml()`, new `.vopt`/`.vnm`/`.vmeta` CSS + a `.vopt`-scoped copy of the existing `.flag`/`.flag.on` chip styling used by the dashboard's Paid/Loc indicators). Wired into `wireComboAdd`'s dropdown renderer, which already backs `Vehicle_Issue`'s "+ Add…" box — that field's plain-text rendering is unaffected since `vendorOptionHtml` only renders the rich row when an option carries vendor info (`.vinfo`), falling back to the plain label otherwise.
- **Added a dynamic `hint` line under the field** (buildField's multiselect branch now supports `f.hint` as a function of `state.record`, not just a static string) explaining which mode is active — "showing all vendors," "showing vendors within 8 km…," or a fallback message if location data isn't available yet. Added `"Preferred_Vendor"` to `RERENDER` so flipping the radio immediately refreshes this hint and the option pool.
- **Vendor phone/priority/online-status/location come from `vendors_Report` via a new `CONFIG.vendorFieldCandidates`** (several guessed field names tried in order per attribute, same defensive pattern the existing label lookups already use) — **none of these are confirmed live** (see §4); everything degrades to blank/"Unknown" rather than erroring if the real report doesn't expose a given attribute under any of the guessed names.
- **Also implemented the Vendors save format this file had already documented as a known gap** (§3: `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"`, e.g. `"[3] Vendor One | +917210036790 | Online"`) — `formatVendorForSave()` composes it in `buildPayload` from the same `vendors_Report` lookup, degrading to the plain name if priority/phone/status aren't found. Symmetrically, `openTicket()` now strips a previously-saved composite string back to the plain name (reusing the existing `vendorDisplayName()` parser from the dashboard's Work In Progress rows) so reopening an already-assigned ticket shows a normal name-only chip instead of the raw composite string, and re-composes it fresh on the next save.

### 2026-07-30 (later still) — Scroll-to-top fix, 17-decimal lat/long, default Preferred_Vendor, keyboard-navigable combos
- **Fixed: advancing a step (Save & Continue / Previous / clicking a stepper step) left the page scrolled to wherever the agent had scrolled down to reach the button, instead of jumping to the top of the new step.** `window.scrollTo(0,0)` was already being called in all four navigation paths (`showView`, `saveStep`'s advance branch, `prevStep`, `jumpTo`) but only ever resets *this document's own* scroll — since the widget runs inside a Zoho Creator-hosted iframe, if Creator auto-sizes that iframe to full content height (no scrollbar of its own), the scrollbar the agent actually sees and uses belongs to the *outer* Creator page, which a same-document `scrollTo` can't touch. Replaced all four calls with a new `scrollToTop()` that also resets `document.documentElement`/`body.scrollTop` (older-engine fallback) and best-effort reaches `window.parent`/`window.top` (wrapped in try/catch — only takes effect if same-origin; safely no-ops under the normal cross-origin widget embed instead of throwing). **If the outer Creator page is on a different origin than this widget (the typical setup), the parent-scroll attempts will silently no-op** and only this document's own scroll resets — flagging in §4 as the fallback explanation if the symptom persists, since there's no evidence in this codebase of a Zoho SDK method for notifying the host page of a scroll/resize intent.
- **Lat/long precision cap raised from 6 to 17 decimal places, per explicit request.** `roundCoord()` used to do `Math.round(n*1e6)/1e6` as a guessed-safe cap after a live "Longitude has exceeded its maximum digits" error (see §4 — still not confirmed against the field's real configured limit). Re-implemented via `Number(n.toFixed(17))` instead of extending the same `Math.round(n*1e17)/1e17` pattern to 17 decimals — that multiply-based approach overflows `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` for any coordinate with 2+ integer digits (e.g. `77.xxx`) and would silently round *incorrectly* rather than to the requested precision. In practice this is close to a pass-through for any real GPS coordinate, since a JS double only carries ~15–17 significant digits total regardless of how many decimal places are requested.

### 2026-07-30 (even later) — Coordinate save now retries at lower precision instead of failing outright
- **Confirmed live (screenshot) that "…exceeded its maximum digits" still fires at 17 decimal places** (`23.1234567890123455` → 17 total digits after rounding → rejected). This is a hard server-side limit on the `Latitude`/`Longitude` `Decimal` field's configured precision in Zoho Studio — **not** something client-side rounding alone can satisfy without knowing the field's exact real limit, which still isn't confirmed (see §4). Raising the *requested* cap to 17 (previous entry) was correct per the ask, but doesn't help if Zoho's actual field can't hold that many digits at all.
- Instead of guessing a single fixed lower cap (wrong for some environments, needlessly lossy for others), added `roundCoord(n, decimals)` (now takes an optional precision override — all existing call sites are unchanged, they still default to 17) and a new `saveWithCoordFallback(payload, saveFn)`: on a save rejected specifically for "maximum digits" (matched via `/maximum digits/i` against the thrown error's message — both `apiAdd`/`apiUpdate` already surface Zoho's raw error text that way), it re-rounds `COORD_FIELDS` down to the next level in `COORD_PRECISION_FALLBACKS = [17,14,12,10,8,6,4,2,0]` and retries, repeating until one succeeds or every level has failed (in which case the last error is re-thrown as before). Any other error type is re-thrown immediately, unretried.
- Wired into every place a ticket gets saved: `saveStep()` (both the first `addTicket` and every subsequent `updateTicket`) and `cancelWizard()`'s best-effort save (`closeTicketAction()`, the third original call site, was removed the same day — see the Final Closure entry below; `saveClosure()` doesn't need this wrapper since it never touches coordinate fields). Whichever precision level finally succeeds is what actually gets saved — there's no separate "what we tried to send" vs. "what's actually stored" drift, since `payload`'s coordinate fields are mutated in place between attempts.
- Tested the retry loop in isolation (mock save that only accepts ≤8 total digits, a save that never succeeds, and an unrelated error type) — degrades through the fallback levels correctly, re-throws after exhausting them, and doesn't retry unrelated failures.
- **Still open**: the real per-environment digit limit is unknown, so a save might now take a few silent retries (visible only in the console) before succeeding, or in the worst case save at `0` decimal places (whole-number lat/long) if the field's limit is that strict. If saves are consistently landing at very low precision, that's a strong signal to check the field's actual configured precision in Zoho Studio directly rather than continuing to guess.
- **`Preferred_Vendor` now defaults to "No"** instead of rendering with neither radio option selected (and saving as blank if the agent never touches it). Added generic `f.default` support to the radio branch of `buildField` (mirroring the pattern the `select` branch already had) and set `default:"No"` on the field. Since `collectStep` reads whichever `.opt` carries the `.sel` class at save time, a defaulted-but-untouched radio now correctly saves as `"No"`, not `""`.
- **The searchable combo dropdowns (`wireCombo` — Client/Vehicle; `wireComboAdd` — Vehicle_Issue/Vendors) are now keyboard-navigable**: ArrowDown/ArrowUp move a highlighted option (new `.combo-opt.hi` state, same visual as the existing hover style), Enter picks whichever option is currently highlighted, Escape closes the dropdown. The top match is auto-highlighted whenever the list (re)renders (on focus or as you type), so typing a few characters and hitting Enter immediately picks the best match without needing an ArrowDown first — the same convention as most autocomplete UIs. Picking via keyboard reuses the exact same `pick()` logic the existing mousedown handler already called (refactored out, not duplicated), so RERENDER side effects, chip-adding, etc. all behave identically regardless of input method.

### 2026-07-30 (even later still) — Final Closure: a dedicated screen replacing the old in-wizard Close button
Built from a Back-Office "Final Closure RSR & Tow" mock (Actor: Back-Office Team; Purpose: close the ticket and share all data with the Rescue team). Two interpretation choices were confirmed with the user before building this (rather than guessed):
- The mock's field list shows a 3-option `Closure_Status` (Not Converted/Cancelled/Completed), but its own validation table has separate "CANCELLED BILLABLE"/"CANCELLED NOT BILLABLE" rows — both with the *identical* fee condition. **Confirmed: keep 3 options** — billable-ness isn't tracked as its own saved value, since the validation behavior doesn't actually differ between the two.
- The mock's own footer note says this screen needs "a permanent button... next to the next action button" (i.e. inside the wizard), which seemed to contradict the initial ask ("don't give close task button inside form, give in the report"). **Confirmed: both** — the old in-wizard Close button is gone, but a *new*, different one (opening this same rich screen, not the old minimal one) sits next to Save & Continue, in addition to a new dashboard row action.

**Removed:**
- The old `closure` STAGE (`branch:"final"`, just `__summary` + `Remarks`, wrote `Status="CLOSED"` as the wizard's own last step) — deleted from `STAGES` entirely. `buildPath()` no longer concatenates a `fin`/`"final"` branch; the linear wizard path now ends at `payment`.
- The old `#closeBtn` ("Close Ticket", danger-styled, saved the *current* step's fields + hardcoded `Status="CLOSED"`) and its handler `closeTicketAction()` — both deleted. `saveStep()`'s now-unreachable `step.branch==="final"` special case was removed too.
- `summaryHtml()` — repurposed into the richer `closureSummaryHtml()` below (same `t:"summary"` field-type plumbing in `buildField`/`collectStep`/`buildPayload` reused, not duplicated).

**Added — a whole new top-level view, separate from the step wizard:**
- `#closureView` (new HTML container, reuses `.wizard`/`.wiz-top`/`.form-card` styling) + `showView()` extended with a third `"closure"` state, toggling `#closureView` and a new set of bar buttons (`closureCancelBtn`/`closureSaveBtn`) alongside the existing wizard ones (`prevBtn`/`cancelBtn`/`saveBtn`/new `wizClosureBtn`).
- `openClosure(rec)` / `renderClosure()` — loads a ticket directly into this screen **without touching `state.path`/`stepIndex`** (unlike `openTicket`), so it works regardless of which step the ticket is actually on or how far the stepper has progressed — matching the mock's "Back-office team can open this page at anytime during the Task timeline."
- `CLOSURE_FIELDS` (a new field-array schema, same shape as a STAGE's `fields`, rendered via the existing `renderFields()`/`buildField()` — now accepting an optional `targetId` param so it can render into `#closureFields` instead of the wizard's own `#fields`): `Refund_Due`, `Refund_Reason`/`Refund_Amount` (shown+required only when a refund's due, reusing the existing `REJECT_REASONS` list — the mock didn't specify a separate reason list), `Closure_Status` (the 3 options above), `Closure_Reason` (shown+required only for Cancelled/Not Converted, per the mock's own note), `Remarks` (optional, per the mock), `Cx_Feedback_Score` (1–10 radio, required only when Completed), `Cx_Feedback_Text` (optional), `B2B_GST_Invoice_Required`/`GST_Number` (GST_Number shown when B2B=Yes, required only when B2B=Yes *and* Completed), and `Closure_Extra_Photo` (the mock's "option of uploading more" photos affordance).
- `closureSummaryHtml()` (replacing `summaryHtml()`) — read-only display cards for Customer Details / Service Summary (incl. distances, ETA, service time) / Fee Collected / Photos / Mechanic-Vendor, all pulled from data already on the record — no new fields needed for these, just a richer read-only view. **Photos only show an "Uploaded" presence indicator, not an actual preview** — this app has no existing mechanism anywhere to read back a file field's URL after upload, so this doesn't regress anything, it just doesn't add capability the codebase didn't already have elsewhere.
- `computeClosureTSF(r)` — ports the mock's `TSF=(BF+BALF)-(REFUNDBF+REFUNDBALF)` formula (`BALF` = this app's existing `Remaining_Fee`; the mock's separate `REFUNDBF`/`REFUNDBALF` split isn't reflected in a real field here, since the screen only has one `Refund_Amount` box — treated as their combined total). Applied by `saveClosure()` only when `Closure_Status` is Completed or Cancelled (both rows in the mock's table use this identical formula); Not Converted has no fee condition at all, so `Total_Service_Fee` is left untouched in that case.
- `saveClosure()` — validates via `collectStep(true)`, applies the TSF formula where applicable, writes `Refund_Due`/`Refund_Reason`/`Refund_Amount`/`Closure_Status`/`Closure_Reason`/`Remarks`/`Cx_Feedback_Score`/`Cx_Feedback_Text`/`B2B_GST_Invoice_Required`/`GST_Number`/`Status="CLOSED"`, uploads `Closure_Extra_Photo` best-effort, then returns to the dashboard ("Live Cases", i.e. `backToDash()`) — matching the mock's own closing instruction. `cancelClosure()` (Back to Dashboard / the header's X) is a plain navigation, not a save, since nothing's touched the live record until Close Task is actually clicked.
- **New dashboard row action**: `rowHtmlWithClose(r)` wraps `rowHtml(r)`'s existing row button with a separate *sibling* "Close" icon-button (new `.rrow-wrap`/`.rrow-close-btn` CSS — a button can't nest inside another button, so this wraps rather than modifies `rowHtml` itself) — dashboard rows only; the search dropdown still renders plain `rowHtml(r)`, unchanged. **Clicking the row itself still opens the edit wizard exactly as before** — this was an explicit requirement, verified by leaving `renderReportLists()`'s existing `.rrow` click-wiring completely untouched and only adding a new, separate wiring block for `.rrow-close-btn`.
- **Made `f.req` support a `(record)=>boolean` function**, not just a static boolean (new `isReq(f)` helper, used everywhere `collectStep`/`buildField` check required-ness) — needed for `Cx_Feedback_Score`/`GST_Number`, whose required-ness depends on `Closure_Status`. Existing static-boolean `req:true` fields elsewhere are unaffected.
- **`collectStep()`/`renderFields()` are now shared between the wizard and the closure screen** — `collectStep` sources from `CLOSURE_FIELDS` when `state.view==="closure"`, else the current wizard step's fields, same function either way. Radio-field changes that force a re-render (`RERENDER`, now including `Refund_Due`/`Closure_Status`/`B2B_GST_Invoice_Required`) route through a new `rerenderCurrentView()` instead of always assuming the wizard.
- Verified the validation/visibility logic in isolation for three scenarios (Completed+GST/no-refund, Not Converted, Cancelled+refund-due) and the TSF formula across no-refund/full-refund/partial-refund/refund-due-but-blank-amount cases — all matched the mock's intended behavior exactly.

### 2026-07-31 — Final Closure summary rebuilt: every agent + vendor/technician/driver field, click-to-edit
User asked for Final Closure to expose *every* field from both sides of the job (pointed at the separately-attached Screen-by-Screen Flow Atlas mock for what "agent side" vs. "vendor/technician/driver side" means concretely — Assignment/Locations/Quote are agent screens; Accept/Reach/WIP/Loading/Drop/Unload/Payment are vendor-technician-driver screens), each editable by clicking it, with a popup to change and save the value — not just the existing read-only glance card.

- **Removed** the old `closureSummaryHtml()` (Customer Details/Service Summary/Fee Collected/Photos/Mechanic-Vendor — five hand-picked, read-only `.srow` blocks) and the now-unused `CLOSURE_IMAGE_FIELDS` array.
- **Replaced with a fully data-driven, editable summary.** New `closureFieldGroups()` doesn't introduce a second copy of the schema — it pulls every non-button/non-badge field straight out of the *existing* `STAGES` array (the exact same field defs — type, options, `when`-visibility — the step wizard itself already uses), grouped by the stage it belongs to: `create`/`quote`/`locations`/`assignment` (agent side), then `acceptance`/`reach`/`wip` **or** `acceptanceTow`/`reachTow`/`wipLoadingTow`/`wipReachedTow`/`wipUnloadingTow` depending on the ticket's own `Service_Type` (vendor/technician/driver side), then `payment`. Because it's the same field objects, not copies, this can never drift out of sync with the wizard the way a hand-maintained second list eventually would — a field added to a STAGE automatically appears here too. Every photo field (`Image_Upload*`, `VCRF*`, `Odometer_Reading`, `Handover_Image`, etc.) is already one of these per-stage fields, so it now shows in its own natural group instead of a separate catch-all "Photos" block.
- **Every row is a clickable button** (`closureFieldRowHtml`/`.crow` — new CSS) showing the field's label and current value (or a muted "—"/"None selected"/"Not uploaded" placeholder). Clicking one opens `#fieldModal` (new, a wide variant of the existing confirm-modal pattern) with **that exact field's own `buildField()` editor** — same input type, same dropdown/combo/radio/file behavior as everywhere else in this widget, via `openFieldEditor(fieldName)` looking the field up in a rebuilt-per-render `CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX`.
- **Save writes just that one field to Zoho immediately** (`saveFieldEditor()`/`collectSingleField()`) — not gated behind the bottom Close Task button — mirroring `buildPayload()`'s own per-type encoding (multiselect arrays incl. the `Vendors` composite-string format, `check`→`["Yes"]`/`[]`, `useId` lookups, coordinate rounding via the existing `saveWithCoordFallback`) so a field corrected here is written identically to how the wizard would write it. An empty value is simply not sent, same as `buildPayload` — this app has no "clear a field" semantics anywhere else either (flagging, not fixing — out of scope for this request). On failure the popup stays open (Save re-enabled) so the agent can retry or Cancel instead of losing the edit; the `#toast` element's `z-index` was bumped above `.modal`'s so an error toast is actually visible while the popup is still up — every other `toast()` call in this app only ever fired after a modal had already closed, so this case didn't exist before.
- **Fixed a latent, previously-unreachable bug** while wiring this up: both `wireField`'s plain `select` `onchange` branch and `wireCombo`'s `pick()` (searchable selects — `Client`/`Vehicle`) called `renderWizard()` unconditionally on a `RERENDER`-listed field change, never `rerenderCurrentView()` (the view-aware helper already added for the *radio* branch when Final Closure was first built). Harmless as long as every `RERENDER` select field only ever lived inside the wizard — but `Closure_Status`/`B2B_GST_Invoice_Required` are `select`-type `RERENDER` fields that live *only* in Final Closure, so changing either was silently never refreshing that screen's own conditional fields (`Closure_Reason`, `GST_Number`, `Cx_Feedback_Score`'s requiredness) — the agent would only see it catch up on some *other* unrelated re-render. Now genuinely doubly relevant since `Vehicle` (searchable, so routed through `wireCombo`) is newly editable from this same screen. Both call sites now use `rerenderCurrentView()`.
- **Guarded a related side effect for the same reason**: changing `Vehicle` normally clears `Vehicle_Issue`/`Service_Type`/`Total_Service_Fee` (correct during initial ticket creation, where nothing downstream has been chosen yet) — but a ticket reaching Final Closure has usually already been fully worked, so silently wiping its issue/fee just because the agent corrected the vehicle from this screen would be a real data-loss surprise, not the same case that cascade exists for. Both call sites now skip that clear when `state.view==="closure"`.
- **Not yet done / explicitly out of scope this round**: `CLOSURE_FIELDS` (Refund_Due, Closure_Status, etc. — Final Closure's *own* fields, not part of any wizard step) were left as the existing plain always-visible form below the summary, not converted to click-to-edit popups too — there's nothing to gain from wrapping already-directly-editable fields in a popup. Also unconfirmed field names (§4) still apply to every guessed field surfaced here, same as before — nothing about this change adds new unconfirmed names, it just makes the existing ones (many already used elsewhere in the wizard, and therefore already exercised) reachable from one more screen.
- **Field link names are pattern-matched guesses, NOT confirmed live** (`Refund_Due`, `Refund_Reason`, `Refund_Amount`, `Closure_Status`, `Closure_Reason`, `Cx_Feedback_Score`, `Cx_Feedback_Text`, `B2B_GST_Invoice_Required`, `GST_Number`, `Closure_Extra_Photo`) — none of these existed anywhere in this file before today. Verify each against a real `Create_Case` record in Zoho Studio and correct here if the real names differ; flagged in §4.

### 2026-07-31 (later) — Reconciling against a "Screen-by-Screen Flow Atlas" spec, screen by screen
User is pasting reference screenshots of the intended flow, one screen at a time ("Action 1: Create Service Ticket", "Action 2: Service Fee Quote & Booking Fee Collection", ...), asking for anything not matching to be corrected — **including field API names, if wrong** — but to ask first whenever a real field name isn't already confirmed, rather than guess. Two rounds so far:

**Action 1 (Create Service Ticket):**
- `create`'s save button now reads **"Create Case"** (was the generic "Save & Continue" every other stage still uses) — matches the spec's own CTA label literally.
- `Vehicle_Status` and `Time_of_service` are now `req:true` (both were optional) — the spec marks both mandatory.
- **Re-added `Alternate_Phone_Number`** (removed 2026-07-25) — user confirmed keep the existing +91-auto-prefix `t:"tel"` treatment, same as `Phone_Number1`, rather than switching to a plain unprefixed numeric field (the spec literally says "Number Field limit to 10 digits", which would have meant a real behavior change from the already-confirmed Indian-phone-formatting convention — asked rather than assumed, since the spec contradicted a previously live-confirmed fact).
- **Added an editable `Remarks` field to `create`** — the spec shows it as a plain optional textarea on *this* step; `quote`'s own `Remarks` had been `readonly:true` since 2026-07-28 with nothing anywhere that ever set it. (Superseded a few hours later the same day — see the Action 2 entry below — once the spec's *next* screen also showed `Remarks` as plain/editable, not read-only: `quote`'s own copy is no longer `readonly` either now.)
- **`create`'s written Status changed from `NEW` to `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`**, directly reversing the earlier 2026-07-28 live-confirmed fact, per the spec's own backend note ("TASK STATUS ON CTA — changes to 'SERVICE FEE QUOTE'") and explicit user confirmation. `NEW` is no longer written anywhere in this app. Since `"SERVICE FEE QUOTE"` already belonged to the *next* stage (`quote`)'s own `statuses` resume-list, `create`'s own `statuses` was emptied (`[]`) rather than also listing it — `stageIndexForStatus()` matches whichever stage lists a status first in `STAGES`' order, and `create` comes first, so leaving it listed there too would have wrongly resumed every such ticket back at Create instead of Quote.
- **Left unresolved, explicitly kept as-is per user confirmation**: the `Client` field stays the real searchable Lookup built 2026-07-28 (optional, `useId:true`) — the spec's "Client Type [Dropdown: Ondemand/Roadzen/Allianz/etc.]" plus a separate "Client ID" looked like the *old*, since-replaced plain-picklist design, but the user confirmed the Lookup is correct and the spec is the outdated/simplified view here, not the app.
- Found (via the isolated `closureFieldGroups()` test harness from the previous entry) and fixed a related bug this surfaced: `Remarks`/`Remaining_Fee` each now legitimately appear in *two* Final Closure summary groups (an editable copy and, for `Remaining_Fee`, a `readonly` mirror) — `CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX`'s last-group-wins behavior could pick either copy depending on array order, sometimes landing on a `readonly` one and silently making an otherwise-editable field's popup non-functional. Fixed `closureSummaryHtml()` to always prefer whichever duplicate is *not* `readonly`, regardless of which group comes first — verified directly (`CLOSURE_FIELD_INDEX['Remarks'].readonly === false`, same for `Remaining_Fee`) after the fix.

**Action 2 (Service Fee Quote & Booking Fee Collection):**
- **The dashboard row action / wizard button for Final Closure is now labeled "Close Case"** everywhere (was "Close Task") — user's explicit instruction this round. Renamed every user-visible instance (`#wizClosureBtn`/`#closureSaveBtn` button text, the `#closureView` form-card heading, the in-progress toast copy) — internal identifiers (`saveClosure()`, `closureSaveBtn` element id, `CLOSE_TASK_ICON` constant) were left alone since they're not user-facing.
- **`Distance_For_Quote_RT_KM` un-locked** (`readonly:true` removed, `recompute:true` added so editing it live-updates `Suggested_Rate`/`Total_Service_Fee` via `calcQuote()`, same mechanism `Total_Service_Fee`/`Booking_Fee` already use) — the spec explicitly shows it as an entry field ("used only for towing Tasks") feeding the TOW rate formula ("& the entered distance"), and the 2026-07-28 lock that made it readonly was already flagged in §4 as breaking every TOW quote (no distance could ever be entered at all) — confirming, not just guessing, that this needed fixing.
- **`quote`'s own `Remarks` is no longer `readonly`** — the spec shows it as a plain optional textarea on this step too, not display-only. Editable on both `create` and `quote` now (same underlying field, two entry points).
- **Explicitly asked about, and answered, four things — all kept as current, no code change**:
  - `Payment_Status`'s real option values: spec showed "Success / Pending"; user confirmed **keep the existing `["PAID","PENDING","NOT APPLICABLE"]`** (and `quoteStatus()`'s `==="PAID"` check) — the spec's wording isn't the real picklist.
  - `Payment_Method`'s full option list (spec showed "Payment gateway (Default) / Direct / Cash / Etc" — "Etc" implying more, unlisted, options): user confirmed **keep the existing `["Cash","UPI","Card","Net Banking"]`, still locked `disabled:true`** — not the spec's list, and not unlocked either.
  - The two "Send Message ... via WhatsApp / Copy Msg" buttons: **no WhatsApp integration exists yet** — user's own words: "we will be sending to whatsapp only but for now whatsapp is not integrated... for now just move to next step without doing anything." Left `Send_Payment_Link`/`Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`'s labels and `action` hooks completely unchanged (still "Send Payment Link & Request Location" / "Request Location") rather than relabeling to the spec's wording, since the spec's two buttons (one for "total fee", one for "booking fee") don't map cleanly onto these two *mutually-exclusive-by-`Booking_Fee`* buttons — added a `TODO(WhatsApp integration)` comment at the `action` hook for whenever that gets built, and to revisit the exact button split/labels at that point.
  - Whether to replace `quoteStatus()`/`canAdvance`'s existing 4-state model with the spec's simpler one (status/navigation decided purely from `Booking_Fee`+`Payment_Status`, no location-request button at all): user confirmed **keep the current model exactly as implemented** — noting for the record this does mean one real behavior gap vs. the spec remains unresolved: the spec says `Booking_Fee=0` should *always* save-and-navigate to the Location page, but the current `canAdvance` still requires `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location` to have actually been clicked first (else it saves-and-exits to the dashboard instead) — left exactly as-is per the explicit "keep as current" answer, not silently reconciled.
- **Not yet addressed** (spec detail, no explicit instruction either way): "Upload Photo of receipt ... *Mandatory if Payment Method is not 'Payment gateway'`" — `Image_Upload` stays `readonly:true` (locked, same as before) since `Payment_Method` itself staying locked/`disabled` means the agent can never actually pick a non-gateway method anyway, so a conditional-requirement on that condition couldn't currently trigger regardless.

### 2026-07-31 (later still) — Action 3 (Capture Locations): Status renamed + resume-routing fixed, new Location_Received_Time, drop-location button
User confirmed the current lat/long-paste-and-split mechanism (`LatLongPaste`/`DropLatLongPaste` → `parseTarget`) is deliberately correct as-is ("location for now will be captured in this format only") — the spec's "[Location field type]" wording does **not** mean a native Zoho Location/map-picker field. Also re-confirmed Drop Location is TOW-only, matching the existing `when` guards already in place.

- **Status values renamed, and — more importantly — *which stage claims each status for resume-routing* was corrected**, both user-confirmed after I flagged the collision rather than guessing:
  - `locations` now writes **`"READY FOR ASSIGNMENT"`** (was `"READY"`) once a real breakdown location exists, else **`"LOCATION REQUEST"`** (was `"LOCATION PENDING"`) — matches the spec's own wording exactly.
  - **`"LOCATION REQUEST"` moved off `quote`'s resume list onto `locations`'s** (`quote.statuses` was `[...,"LOCATION REQUEST",...]`, now just `["SERVICE FEE QUOTE","BOOKING FEE PENDING"]`) — the spec's own screen header says this Capture Locations page "will open when Task status is 'Location Request'", but `quote` came first in `STAGES`' array order so a ticket at that status was previously (wrongly) resuming back on Quote. **`quoteStatus()`'s actual return-value logic is completely unchanged** — it can still compute either `"LOCATION REQUEST"` or `"LOCATION PENDING"` for its own reasons; only which stage's screen reopens for that status changed. `locations.statuses` is now `["LOCATION REQUEST","LOCATION PENDING"]` — both mean "come back here to finish capturing the location," regardless of which upstream branch produced them.
  - **`"READY FOR ASSIGNMENT"` added to `assignment`'s resume list** (alongside the existing `"RSP IRA (INVITE RESPONSE AWAITED)"`) rather than staying on `locations`'s — its own name says "go do the assignment step next," so that's where reopening such a ticket now lands. This also resolves the open question from §4 ("what should READY actually mean") that's been open since 2026-07-28 — the spec's explicit wording settles it.
  - `BUCKETS`' "Ready & Invited" list now also includes `"READY FOR ASSIGNMENT"` (kept the literal old `"READY"` too, for any already-existing record that might still carry it — same reasoning as keeping `"NEW"` in "New & Open" after the Action 1 rename).
  - Verified all of this directly (not just by reading the code): `stageIndexForStatus()` now resolves `LOCATION REQUEST`/`LOCATION PENDING` → `locations`, `READY FOR ASSIGNMENT`/`RSP IRA...` → `assignment`, `SERVICE FEE QUOTE`/`BOOKING FEE PENDING` → `quote`, in an isolated test run against the real `STAGES`/`buildPath`.
- **New: `Location_Received_Time`** (field name + "date and time field" type user-confirmed, not independently re-verified live) — stamped via a new `extra()` hook on `locations`, set to the current moment **only the first time** a real breakdown location exists and only if it isn't already set (`hasLocFlag(r) && !r.Location_Received_Time`) — so it captures true "time of receipt," not "time of most recent save" if the agent reopens this step later. Uses the existing generic `fmtDateTime()` (24-hour, no AM/PM) — not `fmtDateTime12()` (`Computed_Service_Time`'s own confirmed 12-hour format), since nothing has confirmed this new field needs that same special shape. Verified in isolation: no-op when location is blank, stamps once when first present, and does *not* re-stamp when already set.
- **`Send_Link_For_The_Drop_Location` converted from `t:"check"` to `t:"button"`** (matching `Send_Link_For_The_Breakdown_Location`'s existing shape) — the spec shows both as buttons; this had been left a checkbox back on 2026-07-28 specifically because nothing had confirmed otherwise at the time (see that changelog entry) — now it has.
- **Added editable `Remarks` to `locations`** too (same shared field as `create`/`quote`'s own copies — now three entry points for the one field, per the spec showing it again on this screen).
- Same WhatsApp-not-integrated situation as `quote`'s own buttons (see the previous entry) — added a matching `TODO(WhatsApp integration)` comment on both of this stage's send-link buttons; no actual behavior change, they still just flip a one-way "done" flag.

### 2026-07-31 (later still) — Action 4 (Mechanic/Driver Assignment): geofence radius split, sorting, RSP Reject re-routing
- **`CONFIG.vendorDistanceKm` split by `Service_Type`** (was a single flat `8`) — now `{ RSR:8, TOW:20 }`, matching the spec's explicit "8 km geofence... for RSR Tasks and... 20km geofence for TOW Tasks." New `radiusKmFor(record)` resolves the right figure; `vendorsFor()`/`vendorPickerHint()` both updated to call it instead of reading the old flat config value directly.
- **Vendor results are now actually sorted** ("Sort results based on priority & distance from Breakdown location") — `vendorsFor()` previously only *filtered* by radius, never sorted at all. New `sortVendors()`/`vendorSortKey()`: ascending priority (P1 before P2 — lower number wins, matching the flow atlas's own P1..P4 ordering) as the primary key, ascending distance as the tiebreaker; vendors with no usable priority or distance sort to the back of their band rather than the front. **Caught and fixed a real bug while verifying this in isolation**: `Number("")` is `0`, not `NaN` — a first pass silently treated "no priority set at all" as priority `0`, i.e. *better* than P1, sorting such vendors to the very top instead of the bottom. Fixed with an explicit blank/null check before `Number()` (same class of guard `hasLocFlag()` already uses for `Latitude`/`Longitude`), then re-verified: a no-priority vendor now correctly sorts last even when it's the closest one.
- **`"RSP REJECT"` moved from `acceptance`/`acceptanceTow`'s own resume lists onto `assignment`'s** — the spec's header says this Assignment screen explicitly opens on `"RSP Reject"` too (to re-assign to someone else), not back on the Acceptance step it was previously resuming to. `acceptance`/`acceptanceTow` still **write** `"RSP REJECT"` exactly as before (their own `status` functions are untouched) — only where a ticket at that status reopens changed. Verified directly: `stageIndexForStatus()` now resolves `"RSP REJECT"` → `assignment` for both the RSR and TOW paths.
- **Added editable `Remarks` to `assignment`** too (same shared field as the three earlier steps).
- **Not yet implemented — needs a real field name/schema answer before proceeding** (asked, not guessed):
  - "Step 1 - Filter Service Provider based on Service Issue" / the footnote's "Employee list is filtered by service type: Mechanics for RSR, Drivers for Towing" — there's currently no such filter anywhere in `vendorsFor()`; needs a real `vendors_Report` field name that distinguishes a Mechanic (RSR) vendor from a Driver (Tow) vendor.
  - The popup table's separate **"APP STATUS"** and **"LOGIN STATUS"** columns — this app currently only tracks one online/offline flag (`CONFIG.vendorFieldCandidates.status`); unclear whether these are two genuinely different real fields (e.g. "currently busy on another job" vs. "logged into the app at all") or just two labels for the same thing.
  - **"Service Provider Assigned"** / **"Service Provider Assigned Contact Number"** — unclear whether these are new, separate fields from the existing `Vendors`/`Vendor_Email`, or just this spec's way of describing the same chip-display + per-row contact info the current popup already shows.
- **Also asked, and resolved**: whether "agent can see all information... but it will [be] uneditable for all vendor steps" means the linear wizard's own vendor/technician/driver stages should become read-only for the agent. **Confirmed: yes** — the vendor/technician/driver updates these fields directly via their own native Zoho Creator form/app (there is no separate vendor-facing widget); this widget just displays whatever they've entered. Implemented the same round — see the next entry.
- Remaining open items from this round (Mechanic-vs-Driver categorization field, App Status vs. Login Status, whether `payment` also counts as a vendor step, whether Final Closure's vendor rows should also lose their click-to-edit popup) moved to the new **`QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md`** (same folder) per explicit request — a short, current "needs a human answer" list, separate from this file's own much longer §4.

### 2026-07-31 (later still) — Vendor/technician/driver wizard stages are now view-only for the agent
User confirmed: the vendor/technician/driver enters their own data directly through native Zoho Creator (no separate widget exists for them), so this agent-facing widget should show that data but never let the agent edit it. Applied to every field in `acceptance`, `reach`, `wip`, `acceptanceTow`, `reachTow`, `wipLoadingTow`, `wipReachedTow`, `wipUnloadingTow` — **not** `payment` (left editable pending the follow-up question in `QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md` on whether it counts too), and **not** the Final Closure summary's vendor-side rows (same, separate open question).

- **New stage-level `viewOnly:true` flag**, rather than hand-adding `readonly:true` to each of those 8 stages' ~40 individual field defs — those exact field objects are also read by `closureFieldGroups()` for the Final Closure summary (built earlier the same day), so mutating them in place would have silently made that screen's popups read-only too, which wasn't part of what was confirmed. Instead, `renderFields(step, targetId)` now clones each visible field and forces `readonly:true` **only on the clone, only when `step.viewOnly` is set** — the original `STAGES` objects (and therefore the Final Closure summary, and `collectStep`/`buildPayload`, which read the originals) are completely unaffected. Verified directly: rendering `acceptance` produces locked controls in the DOM while the original field object's `readonly` stays `undefined`, and `closureFieldGroups()` still sees the unlocked original.
- **Generalized `readonly` support to every field type that didn't already have it** (previously only text-like inputs/textareas/file inputs respected it): `select` (mapped to the existing `disabled` attribute, same convention `Payment_Method` already used — HTML `<select>` has no true `readonly`), `check` (disabled checkbox), and `radio`. Radio needed an extra step beyond CSS: its `.opt` options are plain styled `<div>`s, not real form controls, so the HTML `disabled`/`readonly` attributes don't apply to them at all — `wireField()` now skips attaching the `.opt` click handlers entirely when `f.readonly`, not just styling them (a `.radio-set.locked` CSS class alone would have looked locked but still been clickable). New `[readonly]`/`:disabled` CSS gives every locked control a consistent muted look.
- The step's Save button is otherwise unchanged — clicking it still advances the stepper/writes `Status`; since nothing on a view-only step can change, this is just a harmless resend of whatever was already there (fetched via `openTicket`, or entered by the vendor elsewhere).
- Not touched: `multiselect` and `button` field types — no field in any of these 8 stages currently uses either, so there was nothing to generalize for them; revisit if one's ever added to a view-only stage.

### 2026-07-31 (later still) — `payment` stage's Status corrected from a guess to real evidence
Three new sibling widgets (`vendorTicket`, `technicianTicket`, `driverTicket` — see each one's own README, and the root `README.md`) were built the same day from a detailed field-generation spec covering the vendor/technician/driver side of the flow this app's own `payment` stage exists to mirror/fall back to. That spec gives real Status values this app had only ever guessed at:
- **`payment`'s entry status is `REMAINING FEE DUE`** (written by the prior WIP/Unloading step once work is done) — added to `payment.statuses` (was `["RSP COMPLETE"]`, itself just a guess).
- **`payment`'s own write on completion is `RSP CLOSED`**, not `RSP COMPLETE` — corrects the 2026-07-28 guess flagged in §4 ("could be RSP CLOSED instead"). `RSP COMPLETE` was never independently confirmed; this is real (if indirect — from the field spec, not a live Zoho error) evidence pointing at the alternative that had already been flagged as the likelier one.
- **`Payment_Method1`'s real options are `["Payment Gateway","Direct","Cash"]`, defaulting to `"Payment Gateway"`** (was `["Payment Gateway","Cash","Card"]`, no default) — this is `payment`'s own field, a different one from `quote`'s own `Payment_Method` (which stays exactly as last confirmed — see the Action 2 entry above; that decision wasn't revisited here).
- Not changed: `Image_Upload`/`Payment_Method` (the `quote`-stage fields) — this correction is scoped to `payment`'s own fields only.

### 2026-07-31 (yet later) — Real bug fixed: vendor-side viewOnly stages were deriving status from fields no widget ever writes

While reconciling the Action 7a mock (WIP - Loading / Cx Reject — TOW) against `driverTicket`/`vendorTicket`, found that five `viewOnly:true` stages here — `acceptance`, `acceptanceTow`, `reach`, `reachTow`, `wipLoadingTow` — computed their `status()` (used when the agent hits Save, to decide what `Status` value gets written back) from synthetic radio fields (`Service_Request`, `Service_Request1`, `Action_field`, `Action_field2`) that `technicianTicket`/`driverTicket`/`vendorTicket` never actually populate — those widgets write the real `Status` field directly instead. Since these stages are agent-view-only but still writable via Save, this could have silently reverted a cancelled or rejected ticket's real status back to its "normal" value the next time an agent opened and saved that stage screen.

Fixed all five to read `r.Status` directly. Also corrected each field's own conditional `when:` (previously gated on the same fake fields, meaning e.g. a real rejection's `Reject_Reason` would never have displayed) to instead show whenever that field itself has a value.

`wipLoadingTow` additionally had the wrong alternate outcome altogether: it modeled a "Cancel" branch (`Rejection_Reason1` field, `Status:"RSP CANCELLED"`) that doesn't exist on this screen — the Action 7a mock's actual alternate outcome is "Customer Rejected" → `Status:"REMAINING FEE DUE"`, sharing `Rejection_Reason`/`RSP_Completion_Time` with Step 7's own Cx-reject (the real Cancel outcome belongs to `reachTow`/Action 6a, a different stage, which already had "RSP CANCELLED" correctly). Corrected to match, and deliberately did NOT add "REMAINING FEE DUE" to `wipLoadingTow`'s own resume `statuses` list (kept `payment`'s alone) to avoid re-introducing the exact resume-routing ambiguity bug already fixed earlier this session (§5, 2026-07-28 "locations step" entry). Also added the matching (previously entirely missing) Cx-Reject fields display to the RSR `wip` stage for parity (`Rejection_Reason`, `RSP_Completion_Time`, `Unresolved_Note`).

See `getRescueTicket/QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md` and each of `driverTicket`/`vendorTicket`'s own READMEs for the matching field-widget-side changes from this same round.

### 2026-07-31 (yet later still again) — Real Zoho field name correction: `Vendors` → `Vendors1`

The user confirmed the real Create_Case field for the Assignment step's vendor-picker multiselect is `Vendors1`, not `Vendors` (which this app had used since early in the build, apparently a stale/wrong guess or a since-renamed field on the Zoho side). Renamed everywhere it's read or written: the `assignment` stage's field definition, the composite-save formatting (`formatVendorForSave` dispatch), the dashboard's `vendorNamesFor()`/vendor-chip display, and `openTicket`/`openClosure`'s own display-name unwrapping. Applied the identical fix to `ticketKanbanGetRescue` (which carries its own copy of this same field) since it was the same wrong name there too, even though that widget is otherwise out of this session's active scope.

### 2026-07-31 (one more still) — Real bug fixed: `Vendors1` is a Lookup, not plain text — vendor assignment was silently saving nothing

Reported live: clicking to assign a vendor on the Assignment step left the `Vendors` field empty on the actual record. Root cause: `Vendors1` is a real (multi) **Lookup** field to `vendors_Report` (confirmed by the user directly), but this app was sending it the old composite `"[priority] Name | Phone | Status"` **text** string (`formatVendorForSave()`) — which a genuine Lookup field rejects, so the save silently did nothing.

Fixed: added `useId:true` to `Vendors1`'s field definition (same mechanism already used for `Vehicle`/`Client`), so it now saves real `vendors_Report` record IDs via the existing `idsFor()` resolver. Removed `formatVendorForSave()` entirely (dead code now — a Lookup field has no room for the old inline priority/phone/status metadata). Simplified `openTicket`/`openClosure` — the generic `useId`/`unwrapLookup()` pass now handles `Vendors1`'s display-name extraction for free, so the separate `vendorDisplayName()` re-parsing step there was redundant and removed. `vendorNamesFor()`/`vendorDisplayName()` (still used for the dashboard's Work In Progress vendor-name display) now extract from a lookup object's `display_value` first, falling back to the old composite-string parsing only for backward compatibility with any ticket saved under the old format. Applied the identical fix to `ticketKanbanGetRescue`.

Also added a new `Assigned_Vendor` field (single Lookup, view-only display in `acceptance`/`acceptanceTow`) per the user's description of the fuller flow: `Vendors1` holds the *invited candidates*, `Assigned_Vendor` should get set once one of them actually *accepts*. **Not yet written by any widget** — see `QUESTIONS_TO_ASK.md` for what's needed (which report it targets, and how `vendorTicket` would resolve its own `vendors_Report` ID) before `vendorTicket`'s Accept flow can send it. The user also described an analogous two-step request/accept flow for the fleet hand-off (a "technicians multi-lookup" for requesting candidates, `Assigned_Technician` set on accept) — not yet implemented, same reason; `vendorTicket`'s fleet hand-off still direct-assigns for now.

### 2026-07-31 (one more still, again) — Assignment screen simplified to match the mock; dead field removed; vendor dashboard-matching simplified

Live feedback: the Assignment screen had accumulated more fields than the Action 4 mock actually shows (which is just the Assign-To picker + Remarks — everything else is outcome data belonging on a later screen). Simplified `assignment`'s own fields to `Preferred_Vendor`, `Vendors1`, `Remarks` only; moved `Vendor_Email`, `Assigned_Technician`, `Assigned_Technician_Email` onto `acceptance`/`acceptanceTow`'s own read-only display (alongside the new `Assigned_Vendor`), since they're only ever meaningful once someone's actually accepted. **Removed `Assignment_Sent` entirely** — audited it and found nothing reads it anywhere, no real notification system is wired to it, and it isn't in the mock at all; it looks like a leftover pre-mock guess.

Also simplified `vendorTicket`'s own dashboard-matching: removed the defensive 3-field email guess (`Vendor_Email`/`Vendor_Email1`/`Vendor_Email2`, `CONFIG.myEmailFieldCandidates`) — since `Vendors1` is now a real Lookup matched by membership (fixed earlier this session), that's strictly more reliable than guessing which text field a vendor's email might be copied into, so `isMine()` relies on it alone now.

### 2026-07-31 (yet one more) — Critical bug fixed: `buildPayload()` still called the deleted `formatVendorForSave()`; new hidden `Vendor_Emails` field

While implementing the user's new `Vendor_Emails`/`Technician_Emails` request, found that `buildPayload()` — the function behind the wizard's main "Save & Continue" button, used by every stage including `assignment` — still had its own copy of the old Vendors1-save logic calling `formatVendorForSave()`, which was deleted earlier this session. This would throw a `ReferenceError` on every Assignment-step save.
